@@ -137,6 +137,21 @@ The type can be selected when creating a case (manual or AI), edited in Test Stu
 
 **Storage note:** `TestCase` intentionally has no `testType` column, to keep existing SQLite databases compatible. The value is stored in `variablesJson._testType` and defaults to `Functional` when absent.
 
+### Type-aware execution engine (Phase 10)
+
+`buildStepsJson(targetUrl, stepsCount, module, title, testType)` always emits **exactly `stepsCount` steps** and tailors the flow to the selected taxonomy:
+
+| Test type | What the generated steps execute |
+| --- | --- |
+| **Penetration** | Navigate → verify the input/form surface → enter a benign XSS/SQLi reflection probe → AI-assert the payload is escaped and CSP/WAF blocks unescaped script execution. |
+| **Security** | Navigate → audit `Strict-Transport-Security`, `Content-Security-Policy`, `X-Frame-Options` → verify `Secure`/`HttpOnly` cookies → AI-assert OWASP Top-10 posture. |
+| **Accessibility** | Navigate → verify WCAG 2.2 landmarks (`main`, `nav`, `h1`) and `alt` attributes → keyboard-tab/focus the primary control → AI-assert AA contrast and screen-reader labels. |
+| **Performance / Load** | Navigate → assert TTFB < 800 ms and DOMContentLoaded < 2500 ms → exercise the primary route transition → AI-assert zero layout shift (CLS) and responsive paint. |
+| **API** | Navigate → `GET` the target and assert `200 OK` + `content-type` → verify payload structure → AI-assert contract schema and latency budget. |
+| **Functional / E2E / Regression / Smoke / Sanity** | The historical four-step flow plus deterministic secondary-section and end-to-end state-integrity steps, so `stepsCount: 6` produces six real steps. |
+
+Live execution: when a case is not intentionally seeded to fail, `run_steps` executes the flow through real Playwright inside a 12-second budget and persists a **PNG live capture** (`data:image/png;base64,…`) per step. Unreachable hosts and deterministic/offline runs fall back to a viewport capture or generated SVG evidence, so every step always carries a valid `data:image/…` payload.
+
 ---

@@ -188,6 +203,73 @@ It resolves the deployed URL (explicit override → selected environment → def
 
 In the UI, open a PR row → **PR auto-regression** → choose environment → **Run PR auto-regression**. The result panel shows pass/fail counts, the target URL, created cases and where screenshots are stored.
 
+### Autonomous CI/CD PR webhook (Phase 10)
+
+`POST /api/webhooks/ci-pr` lets GitHub Actions, GitLab CI, Azure DevOps or any pipeline run the full gate in one call. Authenticate with `x-qasyn-token: cqa_…`, `Authorization: Bearer cqa_…`, or an existing dashboard session cookie.
+
+```json
+{
+  "prNumber": 402,
+  "title": "Feat: BDC commercial loan calculator & checkout",
+  "branch": "feat/loan-calculator",
+  "branchTarget": "main",
+  "author": "ci-bot",
+  "repository": "org/web-portal",
+  "changedFiles": ["src/checkout/loan.ts", "src/ui/Header.tsx"],
+  "previewUrl": "https://www.bdc.ca",
+  "environmentName": "Staging",
+  "createTests": true,
+  "failOnRegression": false
+}
+```
+
+The endpoint upserts the `PullRequestCheck`, maps changed files to impacted modules, auto-creates `PR #<n> · Smoke check …` (4 steps) and `PR #<n> · Regression flow` (6 steps), executes impacted + PR cases against `previewUrl` with per-step screenshots, scores risk with Bivia `/score` + `/set`, runs the Strix header scan, and returns:
+
+```json
+{
+  "ok": true,
+  "verdict": "APPROVED",
+  "prId": "…",
+  "prNumber": 402,
+  "targetUrl": "https://www.bdc.ca",
+  "passed": 6,
+  "failed": 0,
+  "total": 6,
+  "regressions": 0,
+  "createdTestCases": [{ "id": "…", "title": "PR #402 · Regression flow", "testType": "Regression" }],
+  "markdownReport": "## QASyn CI/CD PR Gate — ✅ APPROVED FOR MERGE …"
+}
+```
+
+**GitHub Actions example**
+
+```yaml
+name: QASyn autonomous PR gate
+on: pull_request
+jobs:
+  gate:
+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
+    steps:
+      - name: Run QASyn gate
+        run: |
+          curl -sS -X POST "${{ secrets.QASYN_URL }}/api/webhooks/ci-pr" \
+            -H "content-type: application/json" \
+            -H "x-qasyn-token: ${{ secrets.QASYN_TOKEN }}" \
+            -d '{
+              "prNumber": ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }},
+              "title": "${{ github.event.pull_request.title }}",
+              "branch": "${{ github.head_ref }}",
+              "branchTarget": "${{ github.base_ref }}",
+              "author": "${{ github.actor }}",
+              "repository": "${{ github.repository }}",
+              "changedFiles": [],
+              "previewUrl": "${{ secrets.QASYN_PREVIEW_URL }}",
+              "createTests": true
+            }'
+```
+
+The same snippet, pre-filled for the selected PR and environment, is copy-ready inside the PR drawer under **🔗 CI/CD Webhook & GitHub Actions Snippet**.
+

@@ -225,6 +307,14 @@ QASyn treats visual evidence as a first-class artifact:
 - Self-heal renders `studio-heal-screenshot` and persists the capture on the healed step and the API response.
 - Deterministic/offline runs generate an SVG data URL per step, so no executed step is ever left with “captured during the next run”.
 
+### Visual time-travel evidence lightbox (Phase 10)
+
+Clicking any evidence thumbnail (`studio-step-screenshot`, `studio-heal-screenshot`, `run-history-screenshot`, `studio-run-log-screenshot`) opens `data-testid="studio-evidence-lightbox"`:
+
+- **Single view** — full-resolution capture with Step `#`, action, target URL, status (`PASSED` / `FAILED` / `HEALED` / `SKIPPED`), MIME badge (`PNG Live Capture` vs `SVG Deterministic Evidence`) and timestamp.
+- **Time travel** — `🔀 Compare with Previous Run` (`studio-evidence-compare-toggle`) renders the previous run and the latest run side by side for the same step, with an opacity slider overlay and a **Stable** / **Visual change detected** badge.
+- **Download** — `⬇ Download Evidence` saves `step-<n>-evidence.png` (or `.svg`).
+
 Screenshots are stored as base64 data URLs in SQLite alongside the step/run payload. Keep an eye on database size when running very large suites; the execution engine caps and compresses captures before persistence.

+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { resolveOrgForMcp } from "@/lib/api-token-auth";
+import { biviaScoreAndSet } from "@/lib/bivia";
+import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
+import { buildStepsJson, runTestCases } from "@/lib/engine";
+import { jsonError, readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { parseIntegrationConfig } from "@/lib/integration-providers";
+import { buildPrGateMarkdown, type PrGateBivia, type PrGateImpact } from "@/lib/pr-gate";
+import { runStrixScan } from "@/lib/strix";
+import { audit, readSettings } from "@/lib/tenant";
+import { normalizeWebsite } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
+export const maxDuration = 120;
+
+/**
+ * POST /api/webhooks/ci-pr
+ *
+ * Autonomous CI/CD gatekeeper for GitHub Actions, GitLab CI, Azure DevOps and
+ * any external pipeline. Authentication accepts `x-qasyn-token: cqa_…`,
+ * `Authorization: Bearer cqa_…` or an authenticated dashboard session.
+ *
+ * Flow: upsert PullRequestCheck → map changed files to impacted modules/tests
+ * → auto-create PR-specific Smoke (4 steps) and Regression (6 steps) cases
+ * against the preview URL → execute the suite with per-step screenshots →
+ * build the Bivia + Strix gate Markdown.
+ */
+
+const webhookSchema = z.object({
+  prNumber: z.number().int().min(1).max(9_999_999),
+  title: z.string().trim().min(3).max(300),
+  branch: z.string().trim().min(1).max(160),
+  branchTarget: z.string().trim().max(160).optional(),
+  author: z.string().trim().max(160).optional(),
+  repository: z.string().trim().max(200).optional(),
+  changedFiles: z.array(z.string().trim().max(300)).max(200).optional(),
+  previewUrl: z.string().trim().max(300).optional(),
+  environmentName: z.string().trim().max(80).optional(),
+  createTests: z.boolean().optional(),
+  failOnRegression: z.boolean().optional(),
+});
+
+const LOW_SIGNAL_TOKENS = new Set([
+  "src",
+  "app",
+  "lib",
+  "ui",
+  "components",
+  "pages",
+  "api",
+  "tests",
+  "test",
+  "index",
+  "main",
+  "utils",
+  "shared",
+  "routes",
+  "route",
+  "page",
+  "tsx",
+  "ts",
+  "js",
+  "jsx",
+]);
+
+/** Extracts human-meaningful module tokens from changed file paths. */
+function moduleTokens(files: string[]): string[] {
+  const tokens = new Set<string>();
+  for (const file of files) {
+    for (const segment of file.toLowerCase().replace(/\\/g, "/").split("/")) {
+      const cleaned = segment
+        .replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, "")
+        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
+        .trim();
+      if (!cleaned || LOW_SIGNAL_TOKENS.has(cleaned)) continue;
+      tokens.add(cleaned);
+    }
+  }
+  return [...tokens];
+}
+
+function parseJson<T>(value: string | null | undefined, fallback: T): T {
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(value || "") as T;
+  } catch {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+}
+
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const auth = await resolveOrgForMcp(request);
+  if (!auth) {
+    return jsonError("Unauthorized. Send x-qasyn-token: cqa_… (or Authorization: Bearer cqa_…) or sign in.", 401);
+  }
+
+  const parsed = webhookSchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+  const input = parsed.data;
+
+  const organisation = await prisma.organisation.findUnique({ where: { id: auth.organisationId } });
+  if (!organisation) return jsonError("Organisation not found.", 404);
+  const user =
+    (await prisma.user.findFirst({ where: { organisationId: organisation.id, role: "Admin" }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } })) ??
+    (await prisma.user.findFirst({ where: { organisationId: organisation.id }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" } }));
+  if (!user) return jsonError("No organisation member is available to attribute the webhook run.", 400);
+  const actor = { org: organisation, user };
+
+  const changedFiles = (input.changedFiles ?? []).filter(Boolean);
+  const environment = input.environmentName
+    ? await prisma.environmentConfig.findFirst({ where: { organisationId: organisation.id, name: input.environmentName } })
+    : await prisma.environmentConfig.findFirst({ where: { organisationId: organisation.id, isDefault: true } });
+  const targetUrl =
+    normalizeWebsite(input.previewUrl ?? "") ??
+    normalizeWebsite(environment?.baseUrl ?? "") ??
+    normalizeWebsite(organisation.website ?? "") ??
+    "https://www.cgi.com";
+
+  const tokens = moduleTokens(changedFiles);
+  const candidatePool = await prisma.testCase.findMany({
+    where: { organisationId: organisation.id },
+    orderBy: [{ criticality: "asc" }, { createdAt: "desc" }],
+    take: 60,
+  });
+  const matched = candidatePool.filter((testCase) => {
+    const haystack = `${testCase.module} ${testCase.title}`.toLowerCase();
+    return tokens.some((token) => haystack.includes(token));
+  });
+  const impacted = (matched.length > 0 ? matched : candidatePool).slice(0, 6);
+  const modules = [...new Set((matched.length > 0 ? matched : candidatePool).map((testCase) => testCase.module))].filter(Boolean);
+  const impactedIds = impacted.map((testCase) => testCase.id);
+
+  const pr = await prisma.pullRequestCheck.upsert({
+    where: { organisationId_prNumber: { organisationId: organisation.id, prNumber: input.prNumber } },
+    create: {
+      organisationId: organisation.id,
+      prNumber: input.prNumber,
+      title: input.title,
+      branch: input.branch,
+      branchTarget: input.branchTarget ?? "main",
+      author: input.author ?? "ci-bot",
+      provider: "CI/CD",
+      repository: input.repository ?? null,
+      externalUrl: null,
+      status: "running",
+      riskLevel: "medium",
+      filesChanged: changedFiles.length,
+      changedFilesJson: JSON.stringify(changedFiles),
+      impactJson: JSON.stringify({ files: changedFiles, modules }),
+      logsJson: "[]",
+      testsRun: 0,
+      regressions: 0,
+    },
+    update: {
+      title: input.title,
+      branch: input.branch,
+      branchTarget: input.branchTarget ?? "main",
+      author: input.author ?? "ci-bot",
+      repository: input.repository ?? null,
+      status: "running",
+      filesChanged: changedFiles.length,
+      changedFilesJson: JSON.stringify(changedFiles),
+      impactJson: JSON.stringify({ files: changedFiles, modules }),
+    },
+  });
+
+  // PR-specific cases make preview-URL coverage reproducible across CI runs.
+  const titlePrefix = `PR #${input.prNumber} ·`;
+  const existingPrCases = await prisma.testCase.findMany({
+    where: { organisationId: organisation.id, title: { startsWith: titlePrefix } },
+    orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
+  });
+  const createdTestCases: Array<{ id: string; title: string; testType: string; steps: number }> = [];
+  if (input.createTests !== false && existingPrCases.length === 0) {
+    const module = impacted[0]?.module || modules[0] || "Regression";
+    const definitions = [
+      { title: `${titlePrefix} Smoke check on ${environment?.name ?? "preview environment"}`, testType: "Smoke", stepsCount: 4, criticality: "high" },
+      { title: `${titlePrefix} Regression flow`, testType: "Regression", stepsCount: 6, criticality: "high" },
+    ];
+    for (const definition of definitions) {
+      const created = await prisma.testCase.create({
+        data: {
+          organisationId: organisation.id,
+          title: definition.title,
+          module,
+          criticality: definition.criticality,
+          status: "passing",
+          stepsCount: definition.stepsCount,
+          source: "manual",
+          stepsJson: buildStepsJson(targetUrl, definition.stepsCount, module, definition.title, definition.testType),
+          variablesJson: JSON.stringify({
+            url: targetUrl,
+            _testType: definition.testType,
+            _prId: pr.id,
+            _prNumber: input.prNumber,
+            _environmentId: environment?.id ?? null,
+            _source: "ci-webhook",
+          }),
+        },
+      });
+      createdTestCases.push({ id: created.id, title: created.title, testType: definition.testType, steps: definition.stepsCount });
+    }
+  }
+
+  const prCases = createdTestCases.length
+    ? await prisma.testCase.findMany({ where: { id: { in: createdTestCases.map((testCase) => testCase.id) } } })
+    : existingPrCases;
+  const uniqueCases = [...new Map([...impacted, ...prCases].map((testCase) => [testCase.id, testCase])).values()];
+  if (uniqueCases.length === 0) return jsonError("No impacted or PR-specific test cases are available to run.", 400);
+
+  const run = await runTestCases(prisma, actor, uniqueCases, {
+    targetUrl,
+    trigger: `Webhook PR #${input.prNumber}`,
+    mode: "CI/CD Webhook Gate",
+    deterministic: true,
+  });
+
+  // Calibrated Bivia /score + /set risk, mirroring the gate endpoint.
+  const analysisCode = readSettings(organisation).analysisCode ?? null;
+  let integrationConfig = null;
+  try {
+    const row = await prisma.integration.findUnique({
+      where: { organisationId_provider: { organisationId: organisation.id, provider: "BIVIA" } },
+      select: { configJson: true },
+    });
+    integrationConfig = row ? parseIntegrationConfig(row.configJson) : null;
+  } catch {
+    integrationConfig = null;
+  }
+  const decision = await biviaScoreAndSet({
+    prTitle: input.title,
+    changedFiles,
+    candidateTests: impacted.map((testCase) => ({
+      id: testCase.id,
+      title: testCase.title,
+      module: testCase.module,
+      criticality: testCase.criticality,
+      status: testCase.status,
+    })),
+    organisationId: organisation.id,
+    sources: { integrationConfig, analysisCode },
+  });
+  const bivia: PrGateBivia = {
+    riskScore: Math.round(decision.riskScore * 10) / 10,
+    riskLevel: decision.riskLevel,
+    probabilityPct: Math.round(decision.probability * 1000) / 10,
+    provider: decision.provider,
+  };
+
+  const strix = await runStrixScan(targetUrl);
+  const regressions = run.failed;
+  const status = regressions > 0 ? "regression_caught" : "passed_baseline";
+  const verdict = regressions > 0 ? "BLOCKED" : "APPROVED";
+  const executed = run.results.map((result) => ({ id: result.id, title: result.title, status: result.status, module: impactedIds.includes(result.id) ? impacted.find((testCase) => testCase.id === result.id)?.module : undefined }));
+  const impact: PrGateImpact = {
+    files: changedFiles,
+    modules,
+    blastRadius: changedFiles.length >= 8 ? "wide" : changedFiles.length >= 3 ? "moderate" : "narrow",
+    impacted: impacted.map((testCase) => ({
+      id: testCase.id,
+      title: testCase.title,
+      module: testCase.module,
+      criticality: testCase.criticality,
+      status: testCase.status,
+    })),
+    executed,
+    passed: run.passed,
+    failed: run.failed,
+    total: run.total,
+    bivia,
+  };
+
+  const markdownReport = buildPrGateMarkdown({
+    organisation: organisation.name,
+    prNumber: input.prNumber,
+    title: input.title,
+    branch: input.branch,
+    branchTarget: input.branchTarget ?? "main",
+    author: input.author ?? "ci-bot",
+    provider: "CI/CD",
+    repository: input.repository ?? null,
+    filesChanged: changedFiles.length,
+    riskLevel: decision.riskLevel,
+    files: changedFiles,
+    impact,
+    bivia,
+    strix,
+    targetUrl,
+  });
+
+  const now = new Date();
+  const logs = [
+    { at: now.toISOString(), level: "info", message: `CI webhook received for #${input.prNumber} (${auth.via}).` },
+    { at: now.toISOString(), level: regressions > 0 ? "error" : "success", message: `${run.total} test(s) executed · ${run.passed} passed · ${run.failed} failed · ${regressions} regression(s).` },
+  ];
+  const updated = await prisma.pullRequestCheck.update({
+    where: { id: pr.id },
+    data: {
+      status,
+      riskLevel: decision.riskLevel,
+      testsRun: run.total,
+      regressions,
+      impactJson: JSON.stringify({
+        ...impact,
+        autoRegression: {
+          environmentId: environment?.id ?? null,
+          environmentName: environment?.name ?? "Default environment",
+          targetUrl,
+          createdTestCases,
+          testCaseIds: uniqueCases.map((testCase) => testCase.id),
+          executed: run.results,
+          passed: run.passed,
+          failed: run.failed,
+          total: run.total,
+          regressions,
+          ranAt: now.toISOString(),
+        },
+      }),
+      logsJson: JSON.stringify(logs),
+      commentBody: markdownReport,
+    },
+  });
+
+  await audit(prisma, actor, "pr.webhook_gate", `#${input.prNumber} · ${verdict} · ${run.passed}/${run.total} · ${regressions} regression(s)`);
+
+  return NextResponse.json(
+    {
+      ok: true,
+      verdict,
+      prId: updated.id,
+      prNumber: input.prNumber,
+      targetUrl,
+      environment: environment ? { id: environment.id, name: environment.name, baseUrl: environment.baseUrl } : null,
+      passed: run.passed,
+      failed: run.failed,
+      total: run.total,
+      regressions,
+      createdTestCases,
+      executed: run.results,
+      markdownReport,
+      failOnRegression: input.failOnRegression ?? false,
+      ranAt: now.toISOString(),
+    },
+    { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
+  );
+}

+      previous: data.runs[1] ? pick(data.runs[1]) : null,
+    };

+  function openEvidenceLightbox(input: {
+    src?: string | null;
+    stepNumber: number;
+    action: string;
+    target: string;
+    status: string;
+    capturedAt?: string | null;
+  }) {

+    setEvidenceCompare(false);
+    setEvidenceOpacity(50);
+    setEvidenceLightbox({
+      stepNumber: input.stepNumber,
+      action: input.action,
+      target: input.target,
+      status: input.status,
+      capturedAt: input.capturedAt ?? data.runs[0]?.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
+      src: input.src,
+    });
+  }
+
+  function downloadEvidence() {
+    if (!evidenceLightbox) return;
+    const extension = evidenceLightbox.src.startsWith("data:image/svg") ? "svg" : "png";
+    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
+    anchor.href = evidenceLightbox.src;
+    anchor.download = `step-${evidenceLightbox.stepNumber}-evidence.${extension}`;
+    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
+    anchor.click();
+    anchor.remove();
+  }

+import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
+import { ColumnFilter, matchesColumnFilters } from "@/components/contextqa/table-ui";
 import { CqaIcon, Modal, cqaBtnPrimary, cqaBtnSecondary, cqaInput, cqaLabel, useAction } from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import { formatDateTime, timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
+
+export interface UploadTableRow {
+  id: string;
+  fileName: string;
+  kind: string;
+  sizeBytes: number;
+  uploadedBy: string;
+  createdAt: string;
+}
+
+/** Phase 10 — uploads grid with full per-column header filtering. */
+export function UploadsTable({ rows, readOnly }: { rows: UploadTableRow[]; readOnly: boolean }) {
+  const [columnFilters, setColumnFilters] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
+  const visibleRows = useMemo(
+    () =>
+      rows.filter((row) =>
+        matchesColumnFilters(columnFilters, {
+          "File Name": `${row.fileName} ${row.uploadedBy}`,
+          Type: row.kind === "other" && row.sizeBytes === 0 ? "folder" : row.kind,
+          Size: row.sizeBytes === 0 ? "—" : `${(row.sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`,
+          "Uploaded by": row.uploadedBy,
+          Created: formatDateTime(row.createdAt),
+        }),
+      ),
+    [rows, columnFilters],
+  );
+  const setFilter = (column: string, value: string) => setColumnFilters((current) => ({ ...current, [column]: value }));
+
+  return (
+    <div data-testid="uploads-table">
+      {visibleRows.length === 0 ? (
+        <div className="px-4 py-14 text-center">
+          <p className="text-[14px] font-medium text-[#374151]">No data available</p>
+          <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-[#9ca3af]">There is no data available to display.</p>
+        </div>
+      ) : (
+        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
+          <table className="w-full min-w-[800px] border-collapse text-left">
+            <thead>
+              <tr className="border-b border-[#f0f1f3]">
+                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#6b7280]">File Name<ColumnFilter column="File Name" value={columnFilters["File Name"] ?? ""} onChange={(value) => setFilter("File Name", value)} /></th>
+                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#6b7280]">Type<ColumnFilter column="Type" value={columnFilters.Type ?? ""} onChange={(value) => setFilter("Type", value)} options={[...new Set(rows.map((row) => (row.kind === "other" && row.sizeBytes === 0 ? "folder" : row.kind)))]} /></th>
+                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#6b7280]">Size<ColumnFilter column="Size" value={columnFilters.Size ?? ""} onChange={(value) => setFilter("Size", value)} /></th>
+                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#6b7280]">Uploaded by<ColumnFilter column="Uploaded by" value={columnFilters["Uploaded by"] ?? ""} onChange={(value) => setFilter("Uploaded by", value)} options={[...new Set(rows.map((row) => row.uploadedBy))]} /></th>
+                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#6b7280]">Created<ColumnFilter column="Created" value={columnFilters.Created ?? ""} onChange={(value) => setFilter("Created", value)} /></th>
+                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#6b7280]">Action</th>
+              </tr>
+            </thead>
+            <tbody>
+              {visibleRows.map((upload) => (
+                <tr key={upload.id} className="border-b border-[#f3f4f6] hover:bg-[#fafafa]">
+                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
+                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
+                      <CqaIcon name={upload.sizeBytes === 0 ? "folder" : "draft"} className="text-[18px] text-[#9ca3af]" />
+                      <span className="font-medium text-[#1f2937]">{upload.fileName}</span>
+                    </div>
+                  </td>
+                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[12.5px] capitalize text-[#4b5563]">{upload.kind === "other" && upload.sizeBytes === 0 ? "Folder" : upload.kind}</td>
+                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[12.5px] text-[#4b5563]">{upload.sizeBytes === 0 ? "—" : `${(upload.sizeBytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`}</td>
+                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[12.5px] text-[#4b5563]">{upload.uploadedBy}</td>
+                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-[12px] text-[#6b7280]" title={formatDateTime(upload.createdAt)}>
+                    {timeAgo(upload.createdAt)}
+                  </td>
+                  <td className="px-4 py-3"><DeleteUploadButton id={upload.id} name={upload.fileName} readOnly={readOnly} /></td>
+                </tr>
+              ))}
+            </tbody>
+          </table>
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

 const TOKEN_PATTERN = /^cqa_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,64}$/;
 
 function extractToken(request: Request): string | null {
+  const explicit = request.headers.get("x-qasyn-token") ?? "";
+  if (TOKEN_PATTERN.test(explicit)) return explicit;
   const auth = request.headers.get("authorization") ?? "";
   const bearer = /^Bearer (cqa_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,64})$/.exec(auth)?.[1];
   if (bearer) return bearer;

+/**
+ * Strips an inline `data:image/...;base64,` prefix so remote Bivia APIs always
+ * receive raw base64 (`b64`) even when callers pass a stored data URL.
+ */
+export function stripDataUrlPrefix(value?: string | null): string | null {
+  if (!value) return null;
+  const comma = value.startsWith("data:") ? value.indexOf(",") : -1;
+  return comma >= 0 ? value.slice(comma + 1) : value;
+}

+
+/**
+ * Normalises a raw Playwright PNG base64 payload into an inline `data:` URL so
+ * persisted evidence always renders in <img> tags and satisfies the evidence
+ * contract (data:image/…). Already-prefixed values pass through untouched.
+ */
+export function screenshotDataUrl(base64?: string | null, mime = "image/png"): string | null {
+  if (!base64) return null;
+  if (base64.startsWith("data:image/")) return base64;
+  return `data:${mime};base64,${base64}`;
 }

@@ -0,0 +1,386 @@
+# QASyn
+
+**QASyn is an autonomous quality-engineering workspace.** It maps a web application, authors and runs tests, grounds selectors with visual AI, analyses pull-request impact, gates merges on regressions, and keeps a screenshot-backed evidence trail for every executed step.
+
+The repository is a full Next.js application backed by Prisma + SQLite, with a Playwright execution engine and Articul8 **Bivia** as the multimodal decision layer. It ships with two seeded workspaces (CGI and Acme) so every module can be explored immediately.
+
+---
+
+## Table of contents
+
+1. [What QASyn does](#what-qasyn-does)
+2. [Architecture](#architecture)
+3. [The 19 product modules](#the-19-product-modules)
+4. [The 11 test types](#the-11-test-types)
+5. [Bivia: grounded decisions and self-healing](#bivia-grounded-decisions-and-self-healing)
+6. [PR gatekeeper and auto-regression](#pr-gatekeeper-and-auto-regression)
+7. [Super Admin and multi-tenancy](#super-admin-and-multi-tenancy)
+8. [Evidence: screenshots for every executed step](#evidence-screenshots-for-every-executed-step)
+9. [Installation](#installation)
+10. [Environment variables](#environment-variables)
+11. [Seeding and demo accounts](#seeding-and-demo-accounts)
+12. [Running and verification](#running-and-verification)
+13. [Project layout](#project-layout)
+14. [Troubleshooting](#troubleshooting)
+
+---
+
+## What QASyn does
+
+| Capability | Outcome |
+| --- | --- |
+| Autonomous crawl | Maps a deployed application, records flows and turns them into draft test cases. |
+| AI test authoring | Generates functional, regression, smoke, security, API and other cases from requirements or prompts. |
+| Test Studio | NLP steps, assertions, variables, version history, run history and per-step visual evidence. |
+| Real browser execution | Playwright runs headed or headless with desktop/mobile viewports, retries and screenshots. |
+| Bivia grounding | Resolves an element from a description + screenshot + DOM and returns a calibrated selector and bounding box. |
+| Self-healing | Detects corrupt/empty selectors, asks Bivia for a replacement, persists the healed selector and re-runs the test. |
+| PR impact analysis | Maps changed files to modules, selects impacted tests, computes risk and produces a CI/CD gate comment. |
+| PR auto-regression | Creates PR-specific smoke/regression cases and runs impacted + PR cases against the deployed environment. |
+| Enterprise control | Super Admin can provision tenants, grant credits, seed environments and configure plans without touching SQL. |
+| Evidence-first reporting | Every executed step (passed, failed or healed) persists a screenshot in the step, the run log and the UI. |
+
+---
+
+## Architecture
+
+```
+┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│ Browser                                                             │
+│  Marketing site · Onboarding · Dashboard · User guide (/guide)      │
+└───────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘
+                                │ Next.js App Router (React 19)
+┌───────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────────────┐
+│ app/                                                                │
+│  (marketing)  public pages                                          │
+│  dashboard/   server components → client workspaces                 │
+│  api/         route handlers (auth, dashboard, cron, MCP, webhooks) │
+└───────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘
+                                │
+┌───────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────────────┐
+│ lib/                                                                │
+│  engine.ts          run orchestration, evidence, TestCaseRun rows   │
+│  web-executor.ts    Playwright flow execution + screenshots         │
+│  selector-healing.ts Bivia Smart Locator / self-heal                │
+│  grounding-url.ts   single URL resolution priority                  │
+│  bivia*.ts          Bivia client, boxes, scoring, telemetry         │
+│  pr-gate.ts         Markdown gate report                            │
+│  rbac*.ts           permissions and guards                          │
+│  tenant.ts          credits, settings, audit                        │
+└───────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┘
+                                │ Prisma Client
+┌───────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────────────┐
+│ SQLite (prisma/dev.db)                                              │
+│  Organisation · User · TestCase · TestCaseRun · TestSuite · Plan    │
+│  PullRequestCheck · CrawlSession · EnvironmentConfig · CreditEntry  │
+│  KnowledgeItem · Upload · Integration · AuditLog · …                │
+└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
+
+**Runtime flow for a test run**
+
+1. `app/api/dashboard/test-cases/[id]` (or a plan/suite/PR route) validates the request and checks RBAC.
+2. `lib/engine.ts` resolves the target URL, rewrites `Navigate` steps, executes Playwright through `lib/web-executor.ts`, and asks Bivia to calibrate AI assertions.
+3. Each step receives a persisted screenshot (real Playwright viewport capture, or generated SVG evidence when execution is deterministic/offline).
+4. A scoped `TestCaseRun` is written with `status`, counters and a `logJson` containing per-step evidence.
+5. The Studio reads the same data from `stepsJson` and `TestCaseRun.logJson`, so Step Details and run history always show the latest evidence.
+
+---
+
+## The 19 product modules
+
+| # | Module | Route | Highlights |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| 1 | Overview | `/dashboard/overview` | KPI cards, Bivia token observatory, Quality Autopilot banner. |
+| 2 | Pull requests | `/dashboard/pull-requests` | Impact analysis, risk, provider comment, CI/CD gate Markdown, PR auto-regression. |
+| 3 | Crawl | `/dashboard/crawl` | Autonomous crawl, linked cases, re-import, per-column filters, bulk actions. |
+| 4 | Code Analysis | `/dashboard/code-analysis` | Graphify topology, blast radius, coverage gaps, Bivia analysis. |
+| 5 | Requirements | `/dashboard/requirements` | Requirement library, coverage status, linked test cases, CSV import/export. |
+| 6 | Test Cases | `/dashboard/test-cases` | Full grid, 11 test types, folders, step groups, clone/move/run/export. |
+| 7 | Step Groups | `/dashboard/test-cases?tab=groups` | Reusable multi-step components shared by cases. |
+| 8 | Test Studio | `/dashboard/test-studio/[id]` | NLP steps, AI assertions, grounding, self-heal, versions, run history, screenshots. |
+| 9 | Test Suites | `/dashboard/plans/suites` | Grouping, folders, platform, pass rate, bulk run/export. |
+| 10 | Test Plans | `/dashboard/plans/test-plans` | Suites, schedules, environments, run matrix, reports, recovery settings. |
+| 11 | Environment & Data | `/dashboard/environments` | Environments, test-data profiles, global variables, auth profiles, imports. |
+| 12 | Uploads | `/dashboard/uploads` | Requirements/spec uploads, parsing and conversion to cases. |
+| 13 | Knowledge Base | `/dashboard/knowledge-base` | Indexed product knowledge used by generation and Bivia. |
+| 14 | Team & access | `/dashboard/team` | Members, invitations, roles, verification state. |
+| 15 | Tunnels | `/dashboard/tunnels` | Secure localhost reach for staging-less testing (simulated connector). |
+| 16 | Integrations | `/dashboard/integrations` | GitHub, GitLab, Jira, Linear, Slack, Bivia, browser providers. |
+| 17 | Billing & usage | `/dashboard/billing` | Plans, credits, ledger, 1B-token Bivia observatory. |
+| 18 | MCP | `/dashboard/mcp` | Token-authenticated workspace queries from AI assistants. |
+| 19 | Settings | `/dashboard/settings/*` | Organisation, audits, analysis, bug reproduction, API tokens, Super Admin console. |
+
+> The sidebar exposes the most-used modules directly; the Settings group contains the five configuration pages. The **Super-Admin Console** lives at `/dashboard/settings/organization#super-admin` and is visible only to super-admin accounts.
+
+---
+
+## The 11 test types
+
+`TEST_TYPES` is the single source of truth in [`lib/constants.ts`](lib/constants.ts):
+
+| # | Test type | Typical use |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| 1 | Functional | Business behaviour of a feature. |
+| 2 | End-to-End (E2E) | Complete user journeys across systems. |
+| 3 | Regression | Protecting existing behaviour after change. |
+| 4 | Smoke | Fast confidence check after deploy. |
+| 5 | Sanity | Narrow check of a specific fix. |
+| 6 | Load | Behaviour under expected/heavy traffic. |
+| 7 | Performance | Latency, throughput and resource budgets. |
+| 8 | Security | Authentication, authorization and OWASP checks. |
+| 9 | Penetration | Adversarial exploitation attempts. |
+| 10 | API | Contract, status, schema and error handling. |
+| 11 | Accessibility | WCAG, keyboard, contrast and screen-reader readiness. |
+
+The type can be selected when creating a case (manual or AI), edited in Test Studio, used in the Test Cases Type filter, and is displayed verbatim in the **Type** column.
+
+**Storage note:** `TestCase` intentionally has no `testType` column, to keep existing SQLite databases compatible. The value is stored in `variablesJson._testType` and defaults to `Functional` when absent.
+
+---
+
+## Bivia: grounded decisions and self-healing
+
+Articul8 **Bivia** is the multimodal decision engine behind several capabilities:
+
+- **AI Verification** — scores an AI Assertion as pass/fail with a calibrated probability and an abstain reason.
+- **Smart Locator / self-heal** — given the step target, DOM summary and (when available) a screenshot, returns a replacement selector with confidence.
+- **Visual grounding** — given a natural-language target and a URL, returns a bounding box (`[x1, y1, x2, y2]`, normalized to the 0–1000 overlay canvas) plus a screenshot.
+- **PR risk scoring** — turns changed files, impacted modules and test outcomes into a risk level and confidence.
+- **Token telemetry** — NDJSON ledger in `.qasyn-data/bivia-telemetry.json` meters boolean/choice/ground/score/set calls for the Billing observatory.
+
+**Grounding URL priority** (used by Studio grounding and self-heal):
+
+1. The step's own resolved URL from `detailStep`.
+2. `variables.url` / `variables.targetUrl` / `variables.baseUrl`.
+3. The first `Navigate` step target.
+4. The organisation website (`orgUrl`).
+
+Screenshot capture uses a desktop user agent, a 1440×900 viewport, a 15-second timeout with `domcontentloaded → commit` fallback, and a 400 ms settle before capture. Failures still return generated visual evidence so the UI never shows an empty state.
+
+Configure Bivia with `BIVIA_API_KEY` and `BIVIA_API_URL` (server-side only). The placeholder in `.env.example` is deliberately not a real token and the repository secret scan must stay clean.
+
+---
+
+## PR gatekeeper and auto-regression
+
+**Impact analysis** (`POST`/`PATCH /api/dashboard/pull-requests`) maps changed files to modules, picks impacted tests, estimates blast radius and risk, and stores the result on `PullRequestCheck.impactJson`.
+
+**Gate report** (`POST /api/dashboard/pull-requests/gate`) produces a Markdown CI/CD comment containing:
+
+- verdict (`✅ APPROVED FOR MERGE` or `🛑 BLOCKED — HIGH BLAST RADIUS`),
+- calibrated Bivia risk score and confidence,
+- impacted tests with `✅ Passed` / `❌ Failed`,
+- Strix security grade, score and findings.
+
+**Auto-regression** (`POST /api/dashboard/pull-requests/auto-regression`) accepts:
+
+```json
+{
+  "id": "<pullRequestCheckId>",
+  "environmentId": "<optional EnvironmentConfig id>",
+  "targetUrl": "https://staging.example.com",
+  "createTests": true
+}
+```
+
+It resolves the deployed URL (explicit override → selected environment → default environment → organisation website → PR URL), optionally creates two PR-specific cases (`PR #<n> · Smoke …` and `PR #<n> · Regression flow`) when none exist, runs the impacted + PR cases through `runTestCases` (deterministic mode), persists per-step screenshots, and updates `PullRequestCheck` (`status`, `testsRun`, `regressions`, `impactJson.autoRegression`, `logsJson`).
+
+In the UI, open a PR row → **PR auto-regression** → choose environment → **Run PR auto-regression**. The result panel shows pass/fail counts, the target URL, created cases and where screenshots are stored.
+
+---
+
+## Super Admin and multi-tenancy
+
+Every user belongs to an `Organisation`. A super-admin account (default `admin@qasyn.com`) can switch workspaces and open the Super-Admin Console.
+
+The console supports two POST actions on `/api/dashboard/super-admin`:
+
+### `create_enterprise_tenant`
+
+Creates, in one transaction:
+
+- an organisation on the **Enterprise** plan (250,000 credits by default),
+- Production + Staging `EnvironmentConfig` rows,
+- a `CreditEntry` grant in the ledger,
+- a verified **Admin** user with a hashed password,
+- a `SystemAuditLog` entry.
+
+### `configure_enterprise_plan`
+
+Updates plan and credits, optionally grants extra credits (ledger entry), updates seats/SSO/SLA under `settingsJson.enterprise`, and binds/updates the tenant's default deployed environment.
+
+Both actions are available from the console UI (**Provision enterprise** and per-organisation **Configure**) and are guarded by `requireSuperAdmin()`.
+
+---
+
+## Evidence: screenshots for every executed step
+
+QASyn treats visual evidence as a first-class artifact:
+
+- `TestCase.stepsJson[i].screenshotBase64` + `screenshotMime` — latest evidence per step.
+- `TestCaseRun.logJson[i].screenshotBase64` — evidence for that specific run.
+- Studio **Step Details** renders `studio-step-screenshot` from the step or the latest run.
+- Run history and the run modal render thumbnails (`run-history-screenshot`, `studio-run-log-screenshot`).
+- Self-heal renders `studio-heal-screenshot` and persists the capture on the healed step and the API response.
+- Deterministic/offline runs generate an SVG data URL per step, so no executed step is ever left with “captured during the next run”.
+
+Screenshots are stored as base64 data URLs in SQLite alongside the step/run payload. Keep an eye on database size when running very large suites; the execution engine caps and compresses captures before persistence.
+
+---
+
+## Installation
+
+**Prerequisites**
+
+- Node.js 20 or newer (Node 22 recommended)
+- npm 10+
+- Windows, macOS or Linux
+- Chromium for Playwright (`npx playwright install chromium`)
+
+**Steps**
+
+```bash
+# 1. Install dependencies (also runs prisma generate)
+npm install
+
+# 2. Create the local environment file
+cp .env.example .env
+# Windows PowerShell: Copy-Item .env.example .env
+
+# 3. Generate a real encryption secret and put it in QASYN_SECRET
+openssl rand -base64 48
+
+# 4. Create/sync the SQLite schema and seed demo data
+npx prisma db push
+npm run db:seed
+
+# 5. Install the browser used by the execution engine
+npx playwright install chromium
+
+# 6. Start the app
+npm run dev          # http://localhost:3000
+```
+
+For a clean slate at any time:
+
+```bash
+npm run db:reset     # deletes dev.db + WAL/SHM, pushes schema, re-seeds
+```
+
+---
+
+## Environment variables
+
+| Variable | Required | Purpose |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `DATABASE_URL` | yes | Prisma SQLite URL. Default `file:./dev.db` (relative to `prisma/`). |
+| `QASYN_SECRET` | yes in production | AES-256-GCM key for integration credentials and tokens. Use a 48-byte base64 secret. |
+| `BIVIA_API_KEY` | for live Bivia | Articul8 Bivia API key. Server-side only; never expose to the client. |
+| `BIVIA_API_URL` | no | Bivia endpoint. Default `https://api.articul8.ai/bivia`. |
+| `QASYN_BASE` | no | Base URL used by the parity verifier. Default `http://localhost:3000`. |
+
+`.env.example` contains safe placeholders. Never commit real provider tokens; the verifier includes a `git grep` secret scan.
+
+---
+
+## Seeding and demo accounts
+
+`prisma/seed.ts` builds two complete workspaces, including test cases, runs, suites, plans, crawls, PRs, integrations, environments, audits, knowledge items and billing data.
+
+| Workspace | Plan | Account | Password |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| CGI (validation workspace) | Pro | `cgi@cgi.com` | `CgiDemo2026!` |
+| Acme (demo workspace) | Trial | `demo@acme.dev` | `AcmeDemo2026!` |
+| Super Admin | — | `admin@qasyn.com` | `QASynMaster2026!` |
+
+Additional CGI role accounts (Admin, QA, Engineer, BA / FA) share the CGI password. Use the account switcher in the sidebar to move between workspaces and the Super-Admin Console to provision a new enterprise tenant.
+
+---
+
+## Running and verification
+
+```bash
+# Type safety
+npm run typecheck          # npx tsc --noEmit
+
+# Production build
+npm run build
+
+# Production server (required by the parity verifier)
+npm run start              # port 3000
+
+# Full 19-module Playwright parity suite
+npx tsx scripts/verify-local-parity.ts
+```
+
+The verifier logs in with the seeded CGI account, visits every dashboard module, exercises real APIs and SQLite writes, and writes:
+
+- `contextqa-dump/full-19-modules-parity.json`
+- `contextqa-dump/local-parity-report.json`
+
+A successful run ends with:
+
+```
+ALL 19 MODULES PASSED — report: contextqa-dump/full-19-modules-parity.json
+```
+
+Additional scripts:
+
+```bash
+npx tsx scripts/inspect-contextqa.ts    # inspect the reference fixtures
+npx tsx scripts/repair-legacy-urls.ts   # repair acme.example / example.com links
+npm run test:e2e                        # architecture end-to-end checks
+```

+---
+
+## Troubleshooting
+
+| Symptom | Fix |
+| --- | --- |
+| `EPERM` deleting `dev.db` | Stop `next dev` / `next start` first; Windows locks SQLite files. Then run `npm run db:reset`. |
+| Prisma client out of date | `npx prisma generate` (also runs on `npm install`). |
+| Playwright cannot launch Chromium | `npx playwright install chromium` (add `--with-deps` on Linux). |
+| Bivia calls abstain | Check `BIVIA_API_KEY` / `BIVIA_API_URL`; grounding/self-heal falls back to generated evidence when offline. |
+| Verifier cannot reach the app | Start production server on port 3000, then `set QASYN_BASE=http://localhost:3000` if needed. |
+| Screenshots missing in Studio | Re-run the case; open Step Details and confirm a `screenshotBase64` exists in the latest `TestCaseRun.logJson`. |
+| Secret scan fails | Remove any real provider token from tracked files; keep placeholders only in `.env.example`. |
+
+---
+
+**Interactive documentation:** open **Help & docs → User guide** in the sidebar or visit [`/guide`](http://localhost:3000/guide). The guide is a single self-contained HTML file (`public/user-guide.html`) with role tabs, a searchable feature explorer, six end-to-end walkthroughs, and an architecture/API drawer.

+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
+import { buildStepsJson, runTestCases } from "@/lib/engine";
+import { jsonError, readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { guardPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+import { audit } from "@/lib/tenant";
+import { normalizeWebsite } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
+export const maxDuration = 120;
+
+const autoRegressionSchema = z.object({
+  id: z.string().min(1).max(60),
+  environmentId: z.string().max(60).optional(),
+  targetUrl: z.string().trim().max(300).optional(),
+  createTests: z.boolean().optional(),
+});
+
+function parseJson<T>(value: string | null | undefined, fallback: T): T {
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(value || "") as T;
+  } catch {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+}
+
+interface ImpactShape {
+  modules?: string[];
+  impacted?: Array<{ id: string; title: string; module: string; criticality?: string; status?: string }>;
+  [key: string]: unknown;
+}
+
+/**
+ * POST /api/dashboard/pull-requests/auto-regression
+ * { id, environmentId?, targetUrl?, createTests? }
+ *
+ * Runs the PR's impacted regression tests plus freshly generated PR-specific
+ * smoke/regression cases against the selected deployed environment. Every
+ * executed step persists screenshot evidence through `runTestCases`, and the
+ * PullRequestCheck row is updated with the verdict, counts and run metadata.
+ */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("prRun");
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const { ctx } = guard;
+
+  const parsed = autoRegressionSchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+  const input = parsed.data;
+
+  const pr = await prisma.pullRequestCheck.findFirst({ where: { id: input.id, organisationId: ctx.org.id } });
+  if (!pr) return jsonError("Pull request not found.", 404);
+
+  const environment = input.environmentId
+    ? await prisma.environmentConfig.findFirst({
+        where: { id: input.environmentId, organisationId: ctx.org.id },
+      })
+    : (await prisma.environmentConfig.findFirst({
+        where: { organisationId: ctx.org.id, isDefault: true },
+      })) ??
+      (await prisma.environmentConfig.findFirst({
+        where: { organisationId: ctx.org.id },
+        orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
+      }));
+
+  const targetUrl =
+    normalizeWebsite(input.targetUrl ?? "") ??
+    normalizeWebsite(environment?.baseUrl ?? "") ??
+    normalizeWebsite(ctx.org.website ?? "") ??
+    normalizeWebsite(pr.externalUrl ?? "") ??
+    "https://www.cgi.com";
+
+  const impact = parseJson<ImpactShape>(pr.impactJson, {});
+  const impacted = Array.isArray(impact.impacted) ? impact.impacted : [];
+  const impactedIds = impacted.map((test) => test.id).filter(Boolean);
+  const modules = [...new Set((impact.modules ?? []).filter(Boolean))];
+
+  const existingByModule = await prisma.testCase.findMany({
+    where: {
+      organisationId: ctx.org.id,
+      ...(impactedIds.length ? { id: { in: impactedIds } } : modules.length ? { module: { in: modules } } : {}),
+    },
+    orderBy: [{ criticality: "asc" }, { createdAt: "desc" }],
+    take: 10,
+  });
+
+  // PR-specific cases make the deployed-URL coverage reproducible across runs.
+  const titlePrefix = `PR #${pr.prNumber} ·`;
+  const existingPrCases = await prisma.testCase.findMany({
+    where: { organisationId: ctx.org.id, title: { startsWith: titlePrefix } },
+    orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
+  });
+
+  const createdTestCases: Array<{ id: string; title: string; testType: string }> = [];
+  if (input.createTests !== false && existingPrCases.length === 0) {
+    const module = modules[0] || existingByModule[0]?.module || "Regression";
+    const definitions = [
+      {
+        title: `${titlePrefix} Smoke check on ${environment?.name ?? "deployed environment"}`,
+        testType: "Smoke",
+        criticality: "high",
+        stepsCount: 4,
+      },
+      {
+        title: `${titlePrefix} Regression flow`,
+        testType: "Regression",
+        criticality: "high",
+        stepsCount: 6,
+      },
+    ];
+    for (const definition of definitions) {
+      const created = await prisma.testCase.create({
+        data: {
+          organisationId: ctx.org.id,
+          title: definition.title,
+          module,
+          criticality: definition.criticality,
+          status: "passing",
+          stepsCount: definition.stepsCount,
+          source: "manual",
+          stepsJson: buildStepsJson(targetUrl, definition.stepsCount, module, definition.title),
+          variablesJson: JSON.stringify({
+            url: targetUrl,
+            _testType: definition.testType,
+            _prId: pr.id,
+            _prNumber: pr.prNumber,
+            _environmentId: environment?.id ?? null,
+          }),
+        },
+      });
+      createdTestCases.push({ id: created.id, title: created.title, testType: definition.testType });
+    }
+  }
+
+  const casesToRun = [...existingByModule, ...(existingPrCases.length > 0 ? existingPrCases : [])];
+  if (createdTestCases.length > 0) {
+    const created = await prisma.testCase.findMany({
+      where: { id: { in: createdTestCases.map((test) => test.id) } },
+    });
+    casesToRun.push(...created);
+  }
+
+  // De-duplicate while preserving the impacted-first ordering.
+  const uniqueCases = [...new Map(casesToRun.map((test) => [test.id, test])).values()];
+  if (uniqueCases.length === 0) {
+    return jsonError("No impacted or PR-specific test cases are available to run.", 400);
+  }
+
+  const run = await runTestCases(prisma, ctx, uniqueCases, {
+    targetUrl,
+    trigger: `PR #${pr.prNumber}`,
+    mode: `PR Auto-Regression · ${environment?.name ?? "Deployed environment"}`,
+    deterministic: true,
+  });
+
+  const regressions = run.failed;
+  const status = regressions > 0 ? "regression_caught" : "passed_baseline";
+  const now = new Date();
+  const logs = parseJson<Array<{ at: string; level: string; message: string }>>(pr.logsJson, []);
+  logs.push(
+    {
+      at: now.toISOString(),
+      level: "info",
+      message: `Auto-regression started against ${targetUrl} (${environment?.name ?? "default environment"}).`,
+    },
+    {
+      at: now.toISOString(),
+      level: regressions > 0 ? "error" : "success",
+      message: `${run.total} test(s) executed · ${run.passed} passed · ${run.failed} failed · screenshots persisted per step.`,
+    },
+  );
+
+  const autoRegression = {
+    environmentId: environment?.id ?? null,
+    environmentName: environment?.name ?? "Default environment",
+    targetUrl,
+    createdTestCases,
+    testCaseIds: uniqueCases.map((test) => test.id),
+    executed: run.results,
+    passed: run.passed,
+    failed: run.failed,
+    total: run.total,
+    regressions,
+    ranAt: now.toISOString(),
+  };
+
+  const updated = await prisma.pullRequestCheck.update({
+    where: { id: pr.id },
+    data: {
+      status,
+      testsRun: run.total,
+      regressions,
+      impactJson: JSON.stringify({ ...impact, autoRegression }),
+      logsJson: JSON.stringify(logs.slice(-80)),
+    },
+  });
+
+  await audit(
+    prisma,
+    ctx,
+    "pr.auto_regression",
+    `#${pr.prNumber} · ${environment?.name ?? "default"} · ${run.passed}/${run.total} passed · ${regressions} regression(s)`,
+  );
+
+  return NextResponse.json(
+    {
+      prId: pr.id,
+      status,
+      environment: environment ? { id: environment.id, name: environment.name, baseUrl: environment.baseUrl } : null,
+      targetUrl,
+      createdTestCases,
+      executed: run.results,
+      passed: run.passed,
+      failed: run.failed,
+      total: run.total,
+      regressions,
+      testsRun: updated.testsRun,
+      logs: logs.slice(-20),
+      ranAt: now.toISOString(),
+    },
+    { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
+  );
+}

+const enterpriseSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("action", [
+  z.object({
+    action: z.literal("create_enterprise_tenant"),
+    organisationName: z.string().trim().min(2).max(120),
+    slug: z.string().trim().min(2).max(50).regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/).optional(),
+    website: z.string().trim().max(300).optional(),
+    adminName: z.string().trim().min(2).max(120),
+    adminEmail: z.string().trim().email().max(160),
+    adminPassword: z.string().min(8).max(200),
+    seats: z.number().int().min(1).max(10_000).default(25),
+    environmentName: z.string().trim().max(80).optional(),
+    environmentUrl: z.string().trim().max(300).optional(),
+    trialCredits: z.number().int().min(0).max(999_999_999).optional(),
+  }),
+  z.object({
+    action: z.literal("configure_enterprise_plan"),
+    organisationId: z.string().min(1).max(60),
+    plan: z.enum(PLANS).default("enterprise"),
+    trialCredits: z.number().int().min(0).max(999_999_999).optional(),
+    grantCredits: z.number().int().min(0).max(999_999_999).optional(),
+    trialEndsAt: z.string().min(4).max(40).optional(),
+    seats: z.number().int().min(1).max(10_000).optional(),
+    environmentName: z.string().trim().max(80).optional(),
+    environmentUrl: z.string().trim().max(300).optional(),
+    ssoEnabled: z.boolean().optional(),
+    slaEnabled: z.boolean().optional(),
+  }),
+]);
+
+/** URL-safe slug used for enterprise tenant provisioning. */
+function slugify(value: string) {
+  return (
+    value
+      .toLowerCase()
+      .normalize("NFKD")
+      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
+      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
+      .slice(0, 48) || "enterprise"
+  );
+}
+
+function parseJson<T>(value: string | null | undefined, fallback: T): T {
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(value || "") as T;
+  } catch {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+}

   if (!guard.ok) return guard;

+/** POST /api/dashboard/super-admin — enterprise tenant provisioning & plan configuration. */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await requireSuperAdmin();
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const { ctx } = guard;
+
+  const parsed = enterpriseSchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+  const input = parsed.data;
+
+  if (input.action === "create_enterprise_tenant") {
+    const email = input.adminEmail.toLowerCase();
+    const existingUser = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
+    if (existingUser) return jsonError("An account already exists for this admin e-mail.", 409);
+
+    const baseSlug = input.slug ?? slugify(input.organisationName);
+    let slug = baseSlug;
+    for (let suffix = 2; suffix < 100; suffix++) {
+      const collision = await prisma.organisation.findUnique({ where: { slug }, select: { id: true } });
+      if (!collision) break;
+      slug = `${baseSlug}-${suffix}`;
+    }
+
+    const trialEndsAt = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
+    const trialCredits = input.trialCredits ?? PLAN_CREDITS.enterprise;
+    const website = input.website?.trim() || "https://www.cgi.com";
+    const baseUrl = input.environmentUrl?.trim() || website;
+    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(input.adminPassword);
+    const initials =
+      input.adminName
+        .split(/\s+/)
+        .filter(Boolean)
+        .slice(0, 2)
+        .map((part) => part[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? "")
+        .join("") || "EA";
+
+    const provisioned = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
+      const organisation = await tx.organisation.create({
+        data: {
+          name: input.organisationName,
+          slug,
+          ownerName: input.adminName,
+          website,
+          plan: "enterprise",
+          trialCredits,
+          trialEndsAt,
+          paymentMethod: "Enterprise invoice",
+          settingsJson: JSON.stringify({
+            enterprise: {
+              seats: input.seats,
+              ssoEnabled: true,
+              slaEnabled: true,
+              provisionedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+              provisionedBy: ctx.user.email,
+            },
+          }),
+        },
+      });
+
+      const environmentData = [
+        {
+          name: input.environmentName?.trim() || "Production",
+          baseUrl,
+          description: "Provisioned by Super Admin",
+          isDefault: true,
+        },
+        {
+          name: "Staging",
+          baseUrl: input.environmentUrl?.trim() || website,
+          description: "Enterprise staging environment",
+          isDefault: false,
+        },
+      ];
+      const environments = [];
+      for (const environment of environmentData) {
+        environments.push(
+          await tx.environmentConfig.create({
+            data: { organisationId: organisation.id, ...environment },
+          }),
+        );
+      }
+
+      const ledger = await tx.creditEntry.create({
+        data: {
+          organisationId: organisation.id,
+          action: "Enterprise provisioning grant",
+          credits: trialCredits,
+          kind: "grant",
+          note: `${input.seats} seat(s) · ${input.organisationName}`,
+        },
+      });
+
+      const admin = await tx.user.create({
+        data: {
+          email,
+          passwordHash,
+          name: input.adminName,
+          role: "Admin",
+          avatarInitials: initials,
+          status: "active",
+          emailVerifiedAt: new Date(),
+          organisationId: organisation.id,
+        },
+      });
+
+      await tx.systemAuditLog.create({
+        data: {
+          organisationId: organisation.id,
+          actorEmail: ctx.user.email,
+          action: "super_admin.enterprise_tenant_created",
+          target: `${organisation.name} · ${trialCredits} credits · ${input.seats} seats · admin ${email}`,
+        },
+      });
+
+      return { organisation, environments, ledger, admin };
+    });
+
+    return NextResponse.json(
+      {
+        organisation: {
+          id: provisioned.organisation.id,
+          name: provisioned.organisation.name,
+          slug: provisioned.organisation.slug,
+          plan: provisioned.organisation.plan,
+          trialCredits: provisioned.organisation.trialCredits,
+          creditsUsed: provisioned.organisation.creditsUsed,
+          trialEndsAt: provisioned.organisation.trialEndsAt.toISOString(),
+        },
+        admin: {
+          id: provisioned.admin.id,
+          name: provisioned.admin.name,
+          email: provisioned.admin.email,
+          role: provisioned.admin.role,
+          organisationId: provisioned.admin.organisationId,
+          organisationName: provisioned.organisation.name,
+          status: provisioned.admin.status,
+        },
+        environments: provisioned.environments.map((environment) => ({
+          id: environment.id,
+          name: environment.name,
+          baseUrl: environment.baseUrl,
+          isDefault: environment.isDefault,
+        })),
+        ledger: { id: provisioned.ledger.id, credits: provisioned.ledger.credits, kind: provisioned.ledger.kind },
+      },
+      { status: 201 },
+    );
+  }
+
+  const org = await prisma.organisation.findUnique({ where: { id: input.organisationId } });
+  if (!org) return jsonError("Organisation not found.", 404);
+  const trialEndsAt = input.trialEndsAt ? new Date(input.trialEndsAt) : undefined;
+  if (trialEndsAt && Number.isNaN(trialEndsAt.getTime())) return jsonError("Invalid trial end date.", 400);
+
+  const settings = parseJson<Record<string, unknown>>(org.settingsJson, {});
+  const enterprise = {
+    ...(typeof settings.enterprise === "object" && settings.enterprise ? (settings.enterprise as Record<string, unknown>) : {}),
+    ...(input.seats !== undefined ? { seats: input.seats } : {}),
+    ...(input.ssoEnabled !== undefined ? { ssoEnabled: input.ssoEnabled } : {}),
+    ...(input.slaEnabled !== undefined ? { slaEnabled: input.slaEnabled } : {}),
+    configuredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    configuredBy: ctx.user.email,
+  };
+  const nextCredits =
+    input.trialCredits !== undefined ? input.trialCredits : org.trialCredits + (input.grantCredits ?? 0);
+
+  const configured = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
+    const organisation = await tx.organisation.update({
+      where: { id: org.id },
+      data: {
+        plan: input.plan,
+        trialCredits: nextCredits,
+        ...(trialEndsAt ? { trialEndsAt } : {}),
+        settingsJson: JSON.stringify({ ...settings, enterprise }),
+      },
+      select: { id: true, name: true, slug: true, plan: true, trialCredits: true, creditsUsed: true, trialEndsAt: true },
+    });
+
+    let environment = await tx.environmentConfig.findFirst({
+      where: { organisationId: org.id, ...(input.environmentName ? { name: input.environmentName } : { isDefault: true }) },
+    });
+    if (input.environmentName || input.environmentUrl) {
+      if (environment) {
+        environment = await tx.environmentConfig.update({
+          where: { id: environment.id },
+          data: {
+            ...(input.environmentName ? { name: input.environmentName } : {}),
+            ...(input.environmentUrl ? { baseUrl: input.environmentUrl } : {}),
+          },
+        });
+      } else {
+        environment = await tx.environmentConfig.create({
+          data: {
+            organisationId: org.id,
+            name: input.environmentName ?? "Production",
+            baseUrl: input.environmentUrl ?? org.website ?? "https://www.cgi.com",
+            description: "Configured by Super Admin",
+            isDefault: true,
+          },
+        });
+      }
+    }
+
+    const ledger =
+      input.grantCredits && input.grantCredits > 0
+        ? await tx.creditEntry.create({
+            data: {
+              organisationId: org.id,
+              action: "Enterprise credit grant",
+              credits: input.grantCredits,
+              kind: "grant",
+              note: `${input.plan} plan · ${input.seats ?? enterprise.seats ?? "custom"} seat(s)`,
+            },
+          })
+        : null;
+
+    await tx.systemAuditLog.create({
+      data: {
+        organisationId: org.id,
+        actorEmail: ctx.user.email,
+        action: "super_admin.enterprise_plan_configured",
+        target: `${org.name} → ${input.plan} · ${nextCredits} credits${input.grantCredits ? ` · +${input.grantCredits} grant` : ""}`,
+      },
+    });
+
+    return { organisation, environment, ledger };
+  });
+
+  return NextResponse.json({
+    organisation: { ...configured.organisation, trialEndsAt: configured.organisation.trialEndsAt.toISOString() },
+    environment: configured.environment
+      ? { id: configured.environment.id, name: configured.environment.name, baseUrl: configured.environment.baseUrl, isDefault: configured.environment.isDefault }
+      : null,
+    ledger: configured.ledger ? { id: configured.ledger.id, credits: configured.ledger.credits, kind: configured.ledger.kind } : null,
+    enterprise,
+  });
+}

+/** Reads the hidden test type persisted in TestCase.variablesJson._testType. */
+function parseTestType(raw: string): string {
+  try {
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw || "{}") as Record<string, unknown>;
+    return typeof parsed._testType === "string" && parsed._testType.trim() ? parsed._testType : "Functional";
+  } catch {
+    return "Functional";
+  }
+}

+import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
+import path from "node:path";
+
+export const runtime = "nodejs";
+export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
+
+/**
+ * Serves the self-contained interactive user guide at /guide.
+ *
+ * The source of truth is `public/user-guide.html` so the guide can also be
+ * opened directly (and downloaded) without the Next.js runtime.
+ */
+export async function GET() {
+  try {
+    const html = await readFile(path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "user-guide.html"), "utf8");
+    return new Response(html, {
+      status: 200,
+      headers: {
+        "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
+        "cache-control": "no-store",
+      },
+    });
+  } catch {
+    return new Response("User guide not found.", {
+      status: 404,
+      headers: { "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" },
+    });
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Distinct, sorted option values for a categorical column filter.
+ */
+export function distinctColumnValues(values: Array<string | number | null | undefined>): string[] {
+  return [...new Set(values.map((value) => String(value ?? "").trim()).filter((value) => value && value !== "-"))].sort((a, b) =>
+    a.localeCompare(b),
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Shared substring predicate for per-column filters. Categorical select
+ * values and free-text input both match case-insensitively.
+ */
+export function matchesColumnFilters(
+  columnFilters: Record<string, string>,
+  values: Record<string, string | number | null | undefined>,
+): boolean {
+  for (const [column, needle] of Object.entries(columnFilters)) {
+    if (!needle) continue;
+    if (!String(values[column] ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(needle.toLowerCase())) return false;
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Per-column filter used inside a table header.
+ *
+ * Categorical columns pass `options` to render the distinct-value dropdown
+ * alongside a free-text substring input. Text/date columns pass no options and
+ * only render the substring search. A blue dot marks an active filter and the
+ * popover exposes a one-click clear so no filter can get stuck.
+ */
+export function ColumnFilter({
+  column,
+  value,
+  onChange,
+  options = [],
+  placeholder,
+  testId,
+}: {
+  column: string;
+  value: string;
+  onChange: (value: string) => void;
+  options?: string[];
+  placeholder?: string;
+  testId?: string;
+}) {
+  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
+  const ref = useOutsideClose(open, () => setOpen(false));
+  const active = Boolean(value);
+  return (
+    <span className="relative inline-flex align-middle" ref={ref}>
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        aria-label={`Filter ${column}`}
+        title={active ? `${column}: ${value}` : `Filter ${column}`}
+        aria-expanded={open}
+        data-column-filter={column}
+        data-testid={testId}
+        onClick={(event) => {
+          event.stopPropagation();
+          setOpen((current) => !current);
+        }}
+        className={cn(
+          "ml-1 inline-flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded transition hover:bg-[#eef2ff]",
+          active ? "text-[#4f46e5]" : "text-[#9ca3af]",
+        )}
+      >
+        <svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" className="size-3.5 fill-current">
+          <path d="M10 18h4v-2h-4v2zM3 6v2h18V6H3zm3 7h12v-2H6v2z" />
+        </svg>
+        {active && <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#5b4ee8]" />}      </button>
+      {open && (
+        <div
+          data-column-filter-popover={column}
+          className="absolute left-0 top-full z-50 mt-1 w-60 rounded-lg border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white p-3 text-left font-normal shadow-[0_16px_40px_rgba(15,23,42,0.16)]"
+        >
+          <p className="mb-2 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">Filter {column}</p>
+          {options.length > 0 && (
+            <select
+              aria-label={`${column} distinct values`}
+              className="mb-2 h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2 text-[12px] text-[#374151]"
+              value={options.includes(value) ? value : ""}
+              onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
+            >
+              <option value="">All {column.toLowerCase()} values</option>
+              {options.map((option) => (
+                <option key={option} value={option}>{option}</option>
+              ))}
+            </select>
+          )}
+          <input
+            type="search"
+            aria-label={`Filter ${column} text`}
+            value={value}
+            placeholder={placeholder ?? `Search ${column.toLowerCase()}…`}
+            onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
+            className="h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] px-2 text-[12px] text-[#374151] outline-none focus:border-[#a9a1f7]"
+          />
+          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between">
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              className="text-[11.5px] font-medium text-[#6b7280] hover:underline"
+              onClick={() => onChange("")}
+            >
+              Clear {column.toLowerCase()}
+            </button>
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              className={cn(cqaBtnSecondary, "h-7 px-2 text-[11.5px]")}
+              onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
+            >
+              Done
+            </button>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </span>
+  );
+}

+  async function runAutoRegression() {
+    if (autoPending) return;
+    setAutoPending(true);
+    setAutoError(null);
+    try {
+      const result = await apiFetch<AutoRegressionResponse>("/api/dashboard/pull-requests/auto-regression", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: {
+          id: row.id,
+          environmentId: autoEnv || undefined,
+          targetUrl: autoUrl.trim() || undefined,
+          createTests: autoCreate,
+        },
+      });
+      setAutoResult(result);
+      onUpdated({ ...row, status: result.status, testsRun: result.testsRun, regressions: result.regressions });
+      toast(
+        `PR #${row.prNumber} auto-regression · ${result.passed}/${result.total} passed${result.regressions ? ` · ${result.regressions} regression(s)` : ""}.`,
+        result.regressions ? "error" : "success",
+      );
+    } catch (caught) {
+      setAutoError(errorMessage(caught));
+    } finally {
+      setAutoPending(false);
+    }
+  }
+
   return (
     <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex justify-end" role="presentation">

+  async function provisionEnterprise() {
+    setProvisionBusy(true);
+    try {
+      const res = await apiFetch<{
+        organisation: ConsoleOrg;
+        admin: ConsoleUser;
+        environments: Array<{ id: string; name: string; baseUrl: string; isDefault: boolean }>;
+      }>("/api/dashboard/super-admin", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: {
+          action: "create_enterprise_tenant",
+          organisationName: provisionDraft.organisationName,
+          website: provisionDraft.website,
+          adminName: provisionDraft.adminName,
+          adminEmail: provisionDraft.adminEmail,
+          adminPassword: provisionDraft.adminPassword,
+          seats: Number(provisionDraft.seats) || 25,
+          environmentName: provisionDraft.environmentName || undefined,
+          environmentUrl: provisionDraft.environmentUrl || undefined,
+          trialCredits: Number(provisionDraft.trialCredits) || undefined,
+        },
+      });
+      setData((current) =>
+        current
+          ? {
+              ...current,
+              organisations: [...current.organisations, res.organisation],
+              users: [...current.users, res.admin],
+            }
+          : current,
+      );
+      setDrafts((current) => ({
+        ...current,
+        [res.organisation.id]: {
+          plan: res.organisation.plan,
+          trialCredits: String(res.organisation.trialCredits),
+          creditsUsed: String(res.organisation.creditsUsed),
+        },
+      }));
+      toast(`${res.organisation.name} provisioned · ${res.environments.length} environment(s) · admin ${res.admin.email}.`, "success");
+      setProvisionOpen(false);
+      setProvisionDraft(emptyProvisionDraft);
+    } catch (e) {
+      toast(errorMessage(e), "error");
+    } finally {
+      setProvisionBusy(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  function openConfigure(org: ConsoleOrg) {

+      grantCredits: "0",
+      seats: "25",
+      environmentName: "",
+      environmentUrl: "",
+      ssoEnabled: true,
+      slaEnabled: true,
+    });
+  }
+
+  async function configureEnterprise() {
+    if (!configureOrg) return;
+    setConfigureBusy(true);
+    try {
+      const res = await apiFetch<{ organisation: ConsoleOrg }>("/api/dashboard/super-admin", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: {
+          action: "configure_enterprise_plan",
+          organisationId: configureOrg.id,
+          plan: configureDraft.plan,
+          trialCredits: configureDraft.trialCredits ? Number(configureDraft.trialCredits) : undefined,
+          grantCredits: Number(configureDraft.grantCredits) || 0,
+          seats: Number(configureDraft.seats) || undefined,
+          environmentName: configureDraft.environmentName || undefined,
+          environmentUrl: configureDraft.environmentUrl || undefined,
+          ssoEnabled: configureDraft.ssoEnabled,
+          slaEnabled: configureDraft.slaEnabled,
+        },
+      });
+      setData((current) =>
+        current
+          ? { ...current, organisations: current.organisations.map((row) => (row.id === res.organisation.id ? res.organisation : row)) }
+          : current,
+      );
+      setDrafts((current) => ({
+        ...current,
+        [res.organisation.id]: {
+          plan: res.organisation.plan,
+          trialCredits: String(res.organisation.trialCredits),
+          creditsUsed: String(res.organisation.creditsUsed),
+        },
+      }));
+      toast(`${res.organisation.name} configured on the ${res.organisation.plan} plan.`, "success");
+      setConfigureOrg(null);
+    } catch (e) {
+      toast(errorMessage(e), "error");
+    } finally {
+      setConfigureBusy(false);
+    }
+  }

diff --git a/lib/grounding-url.ts b/lib/grounding-url.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e64ff4f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/grounding-url.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,45 @@
+import { normalizeWebsite } from "./utils";
+
+/**
+ * Studio grounding URL resolution shared by the client and the API routes.
+ *
+ * Priority:
+ *   1. the URL written inside the selected step (target/value),
+ *   2. variables.url → variables.targetUrl → variables.baseUrl,
+ *   3. the first Navigate step's target,
+ *   4. the organisation website.
+ */
+const URL_IN_TEXT = /https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/i;
+
+export function urlFromText(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
+  if (!value) return null;
+  const match = URL_IN_TEXT.exec(value);
+  return match ? normalizeWebsite(match[0]) : null;
+}
+
+export interface GroundingUrlInput {
+  stepText?: string | null;
+  variables?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
+  steps?: Array<{ action?: string; target?: string; value?: string }>;
+  orgUrl?: string | null;
+}
+
+export function resolveGroundingUrl(input: GroundingUrlInput): string {
+  const fromStep = urlFromText(input.stepText ?? "");
+  if (fromStep) return fromStep;
+
+  const variables = input.variables ?? {};
+  for (const key of ["url", "targetUrl", "baseUrl"]) {
+    const value = variables[key];
+    if (typeof value === "string") {
+      const normalized = normalizeWebsite(value);
+      if (normalized) return normalized;
+    }
+  }
+
+  const navigate = (input.steps ?? []).find((step) => String(step.action ?? "") === "Navigate");
+  const fromNavigate = urlFromText(navigate?.target ?? "") ?? urlFromText(navigate?.value ?? "");
+  if (fromNavigate) return fromNavigate;
+
+  return normalizeWebsite(input.orgUrl ?? "") ?? "";
+}
