+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+/* Generated visual evidence                                           */
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+
+function escapeXml(value: string): string {
+  return value
+    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
+    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
+    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
+    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
+    .replace(/'/g, "&apos;");
+}
+
+export interface GeneratedEvidenceInput {
+  title: string;
+  action: string;
+  target: string;
+  status: string;
+  url?: string;
+  stepNumber?: number;
+  detail?: string;
+  capturedAt?: string;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Deterministic visual evidence used when Playwright cannot render the target
+ * (offline / blocked host). It is a real viewport mock persisted exactly like a
+ * PNG screenshot, so every executed step keeps visual proof.
+ */
+export function generatedStepEvidenceSvgBase64(input: GeneratedEvidenceInput): string {
+  const status = String(input.status || "passed").toLowerCase();
+  const tone = status.includes("fail") ? "#ef4444" : status.includes("skip") ? "#9ca3af" : status.includes("heal") ? "#7c3aed" : "#22c55e";
+  const label = status.includes("fail") ? "FAILED" : status.includes("skip") ? "SKIPPED" : status.includes("heal") ? "HEALED" : "PASSED";
+  const title = escapeXml(input.title.slice(0, 110));
+  const action = escapeXml(input.action.slice(0, 42));
+  const target = escapeXml(input.target.slice(0, 120));
+  const detail = escapeXml((input.detail ?? "").slice(0, 150));
+  const url = escapeXml((input.url ?? "about:blank").slice(0, 130));
+  const stamp = escapeXml(input.capturedAt ?? new Date().toISOString());
+  const number = input.stepNumber ?? 1;
+  const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1280" height="720" viewBox="0 0 1280 720" role="img" aria-label="Step ${number} ${label} screenshot">
+  <defs>
+    <linearGradient id="bg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
+      <stop offset="0" stop-color="#f8fafc"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#eef2ff"/>
+    </linearGradient>
+  </defs>
+  <rect width="1280" height="720" fill="url(#bg)"/>
+  <rect x="0" y="0" width="1280" height="64" fill="#0f172a"/>
+  <circle cx="30" cy="32" r="7" fill="#ef4444"/><circle cx="54" cy="32" r="7" fill="#f59e0b"/><circle cx="78" cy="32" r="7" fill="#22c55e"/>
+  <rect x="108" y="17" width="1080" height="30" rx="15" fill="#1e293b"/>
+  <text x="128" y="38" font-family="ui-monospace,Consolas,monospace" font-size="14" fill="#cbd5e1">${url}</text>
+  <rect x="44" y="96" width="1192" height="560" rx="18" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#e2e8f0"/>
+  <text x="84" y="150" font-family="Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif" font-size="15" font-weight="700" fill="#6366f1">QASYN · STEP EVIDENCE</text>
+  <text x="84" y="200" font-family="Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif" font-size="34" font-weight="700" fill="#0f172a">${title}</text>
+  <rect x="84" y="230" width="92" height="30" rx="15" fill="${tone}"/>
+  <text x="130" y="250" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#ffffff">${label}</text>

 /** Executes NLP steps: real Playwright when available, HTTP orchestrator otherwise. */
 export async function executeWebSteps(
   steps: NlpStep[],
   options: WebExecutionOptions = {},
 ): Promise<RealStepOutcome[]> {
   const pw = await loadPlaywright();
-  if (pw) return runWithPlaywright(steps, pw, options);
-  return runWithHttp(steps, options);
+  const outcomes = pw ? await runWithPlaywright(steps, pw, options) : await runWithHttp(steps, options);
+  return outcomes.map((outcome, index) => {
+    if (outcome.screenshotBase64) {
+      return { ...outcome, screenshotMime: outcome.screenshotMime ?? "image/png" };
+    }
+    return {
+      ...outcome,
+      screenshotBase64: generatedStepEvidenceSvgBase64({
+        title: outcome.step.target || outcome.step.action,
+        action: outcome.step.action,
+        target: outcome.step.target,
+        status: outcome.stepStatus ?? (outcome.healed ? "healed" : outcome.fetched ? "passed" : "failed"),
+        url: outcome.step.action === "Navigate" ? outcome.step.target : undefined,
+        stepNumber: index + 1,
+        detail: outcome.message,
+      }),
+      screenshotMime: "image/svg+xml",
+    };
+  });
 }

+          <h3 id="role-title">All roles</h3>
+          <p id="role-description">A shared tour of the workspace. Use the tabs to focus the guide on what your role can do.</p>
+          <ul class="role-can" id="role-can">
+            <li>Navigate the 19 modules from the left sidebar</li>
+            <li>Open this guide any time from <b>Help &amp; docs</b></li>
+            <li>Use the search below to find a feature by name or outcome</li>
+          </ul>
+        </div>
+        <div class="role-note">
+          <b>Permission note</b>
+          <span id="role-note">Admins manage billing, team and settings. QA authors and runs tests. Engineers use Studio, API and integrations. Eng managers read reporting and PR risk. Super Admin provisions tenants across workspaces.</span>
+        </div>
+      </div>

+  <!-- Walkthroughs -->
+  <section class="block" id="walkthroughs">
+    <div class="section-head">
+      <div>
+        <div class="kicker">Hands-on</div>
+        <h2>Six walkthroughs</h2>
+        <p>Each walkthrough maps a real user goal to the screens and API calls that complete it. Expand one to follow the steps.</p>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+    <div class="walk-grid">
+      <details class="walk" open data-testid="guide-walkthrough">
+        <summary><span class="num">1</span> Author, type and run your first test case <span class="chev">⌄</span></summary>
+        <div class="walk-body">
+          <p>Goal: go from a blank workspace to a passing run with screenshot evidence on every step.</p>
+          <ol class="steps">
+            <li><b>Open Test Cases</b> and choose <b>New Test Case</b>. Pick AI Assistance, Import, Record or Manual.</li>
+            <li>Select one of the <b>11 test types</b> in the Test Type field (Functional is the default), choose the module and priority, then create.</li>
+            <li>Open the case in <b>Test Studio</b>. Confirm the Navigate step points at your real URL and add Click, Enter Text or AI Assertion steps.</li>
+            <li>Click <b>Run</b>. QASyn resolves the target URL, executes each step, and writes a scoped <code>TestCaseRun</code>.</li>
+            <li>Open <b>Step Details</b> or <b>Run history</b> and confirm the screenshot thumbnail is present for every executed step.</li>
+          </ol>
+          <div class="callout win">Evidence is persisted twice: on <code>stepsJson[i].screenshotBase64</code> and in the run’s <code>logJson</code>. The Type column always shows the exact type you chose.</div>
+        </div>
+      </details>
+
+      <details class="walk" data-testid="guide-walkthrough">
+        <summary><span class="num">2</span> Ground a selector and recover it with self-heal <span class="chev">⌄</span></summary>
+        <div class="walk-body">
+          <p>Goal: fix a broken or empty selector without guessing CSS.</p>
+          <ol class="steps">
+            <li>In Test Studio, select the failing step and open <b>Step Details</b>.</li>
+            <li>Click <b>Run Bivia Visual Grounding</b>. QASyn uses the step URL, then <code>variables.url</code> / <code>targetUrl</code> / <code>baseUrl</code>, then the first Navigate target, then the org website.</li>
+            <li>Review the returned bounding box, confidence and screenshot.</li>
+            <li>Click <b>Simulate Self-Healing</b>. Bivia proposes a new selector; QASyn persists it on the step, writes a version entry and creates an “Auto-fix rerun”.</li>
+            <li>Confirm the healed screenshot card and the updated run history show the new evidence.</li>
+          </ol>
+          <div class="callout info">If the target site is unreachable, grounding still returns generated visual evidence and explains whether the DOM/screenshot was live or synthetic.</div>
+        </div>
+      </details>
+
+      <details class="walk" data-testid="guide-walkthrough">
+        <summary><span class="num">3</span> Turn a crawl into a regression suite <span class="chev">⌄</span></summary>
+        <div class="walk-body">
+          <p>Goal: build a reusable baseline from a deployed application.</p>
+          <ol class="steps">
+            <li>Open <b>Crawl</b> and launch a crawl for the target URL (or use <b>Quality Autopilot</b> from Overview/Crawl for the full five-phase flow).</li>
+            <li>Wait for the crawl to complete. QASyn records flows as requirements and generates linked draft test cases.</li>
+            <li>Open <b>Test Cases</b>, filter the Type or Module columns, and review the generated cases.</li>
+            <li>Create a <b>Test Suite</b>, add the cases, then create a <b>Test Plan</b> that binds the suite to an environment and schedule.</li>
+            <li>Run the plan and inspect the run matrix; every case keeps its own per-step evidence.</li>
+          </ol>
+          <div class="callout info">Re-importing the same crawl updates the existing linked cases instead of creating duplicates.</div>
+        </div>
+      </details>
+
+      <details class="walk" data-testid="guide-walkthrough">
+        <summary><span class="num">4</span> Gate a pull request and run auto-regression <span class="chev">⌄</span></summary>
+        <div class="walk-body">
+          <p>Goal: decide whether a PR is safe to merge using impacted tests and a deployed environment.</p>
+          <ol class="steps">
+            <li>Open <b>Pull requests</b> and analyse a PR (or open an existing row). Changed files are mapped to modules and impacted cases.</li>
+            <li>In the drawer, click <b>Generate CI/CD PR Gate Comment</b>. Review the verdict, Bivia risk score, impacted test table and Strix security summary, then copy the Markdown.</li>
+            <li>In the <b>PR auto-regression</b> section, choose the environment (Production, Staging or any configured environment) or override the deployed URL.</li>
+            <li>Keep <b>Auto-create PR-specific test cases</b> checked. QASyn creates a Smoke and a Regression case named <code>PR #n · …</code> if none exist.</li>
+            <li>Click <b>Run PR auto-regression</b>. The result panel shows pass/fail/regressions, the target URL and the created cases; the PR row updates its verdict and counts.</li>
+          </ol>
+          <div class="callout warn">A caught regression sets the PR to <code>regression_caught</code>. Open the affected case → run history to inspect the failing step’s screenshot before merging.</div>
+        </div>
+      </details>
+
+      <details class="walk" data-testid="guide-walkthrough">
+        <summary><span class="num">5</span> Provision and configure an enterprise tenant <span class="chev">⌄</span></summary>
+        <div class="walk-body">
+          <p>Goal: onboard a new customer without touching SQL or the database console.</p>
+          <ol class="steps">
+            <li>Sign in as the super admin and open <b>Settings → Organization → Super-Admin Console</b> (or use the workspace menu).</li>
+            <li>Click <b>Provision enterprise</b>. Fill in organisation, website, admin name/e-mail/password, seats, environment name/URL and credits.</li>
+            <li>Submit. In one transaction QASyn creates the Enterprise organisation, Production + Staging environments, a credit ledger grant, a verified Admin user and an audit entry.</li>
+            <li>Use the per-organisation <b>Configure</b> action to change plan, grant credits, update seats/environment and toggle SSO/SLA.</li>
+            <li>Switch into the new workspace from the sidebar to verify its environments, billing and team.</li>
+          </ol>
+          <div class="callout win">Enterprise defaults to 250,000 credits. The ledger grant is visible in Billing &amp; usage and the admin can sign in immediately.</div>
+        </div>
+      </details>
+
+      <details class="walk" data-testid="guide-walkthrough">
+        <summary><span class="num">6</span> Filter, export and operate with confidence <span class="chev">⌄</span></summary>
+        <div class="walk-body">
+          <p>Goal: find the exact rows you need and share evidence with the team.</p>
+          <ol class="steps">
+            <li>On any data grid (Test Cases, Crawl, Requirements, Suites, Plans, Team, Audits, Pull Requests), click the filter icon in a column header.</li>
+            <li>Categorical columns such as Type, Status or Priority offer a distinct-value dropdown; dates and text accept a substring search.</li>
+            <li>The filter icon shows a blue dot when active. Reopen it and choose <b>Clear</b>, or use the toolbar filter chips, to reset.</li>
+            <li>Select rows to reveal the bulk bar: run selected, export CSV or delete (permission-dependent).</li>
+            <li>For operational context, open <b>Billing &amp; usage</b> for credits and the Bivia observatory, <b>Integrations</b> for providers, or <b>MCP</b> to query the workspace from an assistant.</li>
+          </ol>
+          <div class="callout info">Existing search, column visibility, pagination and RowMenu actions stay available — per-column filters are additive.</div>
+        </div>
+      </details>
+    </div>
+  </section>

+<!-- Architecture / API drawer -->
+<div class="drawer-backdrop" id="drawer-backdrop" data-drawer-close></div>
+<aside class="drawer" id="guide-drawer" data-testid="guide-architecture-drawer" aria-label="Architecture and API reference" aria-hidden="true">
+  <div class="drawer-head">
+    <h3>Architecture &amp; API</h3>
+    <button type="button" class="drawer-close" aria-label="Close architecture and API drawer" data-drawer-close>✕</button>
+  </div>
+  <div class="drawer-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Reference sections">
+    <button class="drawer-tab" role="tab" aria-selected="true" data-drawer-tab="architecture">Architecture</button>
+    <button class="drawer-tab" role="tab" aria-selected="false" data-drawer-tab="api">API reference</button>
+  </div>
+  <div class="drawer-body">
+    <div data-drawer-panel="architecture">
+      <p>QASyn is a Next.js App Router application. Server components read Prisma directly; client workspaces call authenticated route handlers. The execution engine is the only layer that launches Playwright.</p>
+      <div class="layer">
+        <div class="layer-row"><b>Presentation</b><span>Marketing, onboarding, dashboard modules, Test Studio, this guide.</span></div>
+        <div class="layer-row"><b>Application</b><span>Route handlers in <code>app/api/dashboard/*</code> with Zod validation and RBAC guards.</span></div>
+        <div class="layer-row"><b>Domain</b><span><code>engine.ts</code>, <code>web-executor.ts</code>, <code>selector-healing.ts</code>, <code>bivia.ts</code>, <code>pr-gate.ts</code>.</span></div>
+        <div class="layer-row"><b>Persistence</b><span>Prisma Client over SQLite: organisations, users, cases, runs, suites, plans, PRs, environments, credits, audits.</span></div>
+        <div class="layer-row"><b>External</b><span>Articul8 Bivia, GitHub/GitLab, Jira/Linear, Slack, browser providers, Strix.</span></div>
+      </div>
+      <h4>Run pipeline</h4>
+      <p><code>request → RBAC → URL resolver → Playwright per step → Bivia assertion/heal → screenshot evidence → TestCaseRun.logJson → Studio UI</code></p>
+      <h4>Evidence model</h4>
+      <p>Each step stores <code>screenshotBase64</code> + <code>screenshotMime</code>. Runs store the same fields per log line. The UI falls back from the step to the latest run, so no executed step shows an empty placeholder.</p>
+      <h4>Grounding priority</h4>
+      <p><code>detailStep URL → variables.url/targetUrl/baseUrl → first Navigate step → orgUrl</code></p>
+    </div>
+    <div data-drawer-panel="api" hidden>
+      <p>All endpoints are same-origin and session-authenticated. Send JSON with <code>content-type: application/json</code>.</p>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method">GET</span><code>/api/dashboard/test-cases</code></div><p>List cases with steps, variables and run metadata.</p></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method post">POST</span><code>/api/dashboard/test-cases</code></div><p>Create a manual or AI case. Include <code>testType</code> to persist one of the 11 types.</p><pre>{ "mode": "manual", "title": "Checkout", "module": "Checkout",
+  "criticality": "high", "stepsCount": 5,
+  "testType": "Regression", "targetUrl": "https://staging.example.com" }</pre></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method patch">PATCH</span><code>/api/dashboard/test-cases/[id]</code></div><p>Actions: <code>steps</code>, <code>run_steps</code>, <code>simulate_self_heal</code>, metadata updates. Returns run <code>log</code> with per-step screenshots.</p></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method post">POST</span><code>/api/dashboard/bivia/ground</code></div><p>Ground a target description on a URL. Returns <code>screenshotBase64</code>, DOM status and a normalized bounding box.</p><pre>{ "targetDescription": "Sign in button",
+  "targetUrl": "https://staging.example.com/login" }</pre></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method post">POST</span><code>/api/dashboard/pull-requests/gate</code></div><p>Generate the CI/CD Markdown gate report for a stored pull request.</p><pre>{ "id": "pr_check_id" }</pre></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method post">POST</span><code>/api/dashboard/pull-requests/auto-regression</code></div><p>Run impacted + PR-specific cases against the deployed URL with deterministic outcomes.</p><pre>{ "id": "pr_check_id",
+  "environmentId": "env_id",
+  "targetUrl": "https://staging.example.com",
+  "createTests": true }</pre></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method post">POST</span><code>/api/dashboard/super-admin</code></div><p>Enterprise provisioning and configuration.</p><pre>{ "action": "create_enterprise_tenant",
+  "organisationName": "Acme Enterprise",
+  "adminName": "Jordan Lee", "adminEmail": "admin@acme.com",
+  "adminPassword": "At least8Chars", "seats": 25 }</pre></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method">GET</span><code>/api/dashboard/environments</code></div><p>List environment configs; the default environment is used by PR auto-regression when no ID is supplied.</p></div>
+      <div class="api"><div class="api-head"><span class="method">GET</span><code>/api/dashboard/billing</code></div><p>Plan, credit ledger and the Bivia token observatory metrics.</p></div>
+    </div>
+  </div>
+</aside>

+  var roleTabs = document.querySelectorAll("[data-guide-role]");
+  var roleTitle = document.getElementById("role-title");

+  var roleNote = document.getElementById("role-note");

+  var featureSearch = document.getElementById("feature-search");

+  var activeRole = "all";
+  var activeQuery = "";
+
+  function applyFeatures() {
+    var query = activeQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
+    var shown = 0;
+    featureCards.forEach(function (card) {
+      var roles = (card.getAttribute("data-roles") || "").toLowerCase();
+      var text = (card.textContent + " " + (card.getAttribute("data-keywords") || "")).toLowerCase();
+      var roleOk = activeRole === "all" || roles.indexOf(activeRole.toLowerCase()) !== -1;
+      var queryOk = !query || text.indexOf(query) !== -1;
+      var visible = roleOk && queryOk;
+      card.hidden = !visible;
+      if (visible) shown += 1;
+    });
+    featureCount.textContent = shown + (shown === 1 ? " feature" : " features");
+    var existing = document.getElementById("feature-empty");
+    if (shown === 0 && !existing) {
+      var empty = document.createElement("div");
+      empty.className = "empty";
+      empty.id = "feature-empty";
+      empty.textContent = "No feature matches this role and search. Try a broader term or choose All roles.";
+      featureGrid.appendChild(empty);
+    } else if (shown > 0 && existing) {
+      existing.remove();
+    }
+  }
+
+  roleTabs.forEach(function (tab) {
+    tab.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      activeRole = tab.getAttribute("data-guide-role") || "all";
+      roleTabs.forEach(function (other) { other.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(other === tab)); });
+      var copy = roleCopy[activeRole] || roleCopy.all;
+      roleTitle.textContent = copy.title;
+      roleDescription.textContent = copy.description;
+      roleCan.innerHTML = copy.can.map(function (item) { return "<li>" + item + "</li>"; }).join("");
+      roleNote.innerHTML = copy.note;
+      applyFeatures();
+    });
+  });
+
+  featureSearch.addEventListener("input", function () {
+    activeQuery = featureSearch.value || "";
+    applyFeatures();
+  });
+
+  applyFeatures();
+
+  // ── Drawer ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  var drawer = document.getElementById("guide-drawer");
+  var backdrop = document.getElementById("drawer-backdrop");
+  var lastFocus = null;
+  var drawerTabs = document.querySelectorAll("[data-drawer-tab]");
+  var drawerPanels = document.querySelectorAll("[data-drawer-panel]");
+
+  function setDrawerTab(name) {
+    drawerTabs.forEach(function (tab) {
+      tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(tab.getAttribute("data-drawer-tab") === name));
+    });
+    drawerPanels.forEach(function (panel) {
+      panel.hidden = panel.getAttribute("data-drawer-panel") !== name;
+    });
+  }
+
+  function openDrawer(tab) {
+    lastFocus = document.activeElement;
+    setDrawerTab(tab === "api" ? "api" : "architecture");
+    drawer.classList.add("open");
+    backdrop.classList.add("open");
+    drawer.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");
+    var focusTarget = drawer.querySelector(".drawer-close");
+    if (focusTarget) focusTarget.focus();
+  }
+
+  function closeDrawer() {
+    drawer.classList.remove("open");
+    backdrop.classList.remove("open");
+    drawer.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
+    if (lastFocus && typeof lastFocus.focus === "function") lastFocus.focus();
+  }
+
+  document.querySelectorAll("[data-drawer]").forEach(function (button) {
+    button.addEventListener("click", function () { openDrawer(button.getAttribute("data-drawer")); });
+  });
+  document.querySelectorAll("[data-drawer-close]").forEach(function (node) {
+    node.addEventListener("click", closeDrawer);
+  });
+  drawerTabs.forEach(function (tab) {
+    tab.addEventListener("click", function () { setDrawerTab(tab.getAttribute("data-drawer-tab")); });
+  });
+  document.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
+    if (event.key === "Escape" && drawer.classList.contains("open")) closeDrawer();
+  });
+})();
+</script>

  * Searches tracked Git files. `ok` means the pattern is absent (git grep exits 1).
  * The error message is never inspected because it contains the search pattern.
  */
+/** Parses a JSON array column (stepsJson / logJson / impactJson lists). */
+function jsonArray(value: unknown): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
+  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim()) return [];
+  try {
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(value) as unknown;
+    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((item): item is Record<string, unknown> => Boolean(item) && typeof item === "object") : [];
+  } catch {
+    return [];
+  }
+}
+
+/** True when the value is an inline image data URL (PNG/JPG/SVG evidence). */
+function isScreenshot(value: unknown): boolean {
+  return typeof value === "string" && value.startsWith("data:image/");
+}

+  check(
+    "PART 9 PR auto-regression runs against the selected deployed environment",
+    autoRegressionEnvironment
+      ? autoRegressionBody.environment?.id === autoRegressionEnvironment.id &&
+          String(autoRegressionBody.targetUrl ?? "").replace(/\/+$/, "") === autoRegressionEnvironment.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "")
+      : Boolean(autoRegressionBody.targetUrl),
+    `${autoRegressionBody.environment?.name ?? "default"} · ${autoRegressionBody.targetUrl ?? "no url"}`,
+  );
+  check(
+    "PART 9 PR auto-regression persists screenshots on every executed case",
+    (autoRegressionBody.total ?? 0) >= 2 &&
+      (autoRegressionBody.passed ?? 0) + (autoRegressionBody.failed ?? 0) === autoRegressionBody.total &&
+      autoExecuted.length === autoRegressionBody.total &&
+      autoExecuted.every((executed) => isScreenshot(executed.screenshotBase64) || jsonArray(executed.logJson).every((line) => isScreenshot(line.screenshotBase64))),
+    `${autoRegressionBody.passed ?? 0}/${autoRegressionBody.total ?? 0} passed · ${autoRegressionBody.regressions ?? 0} regression(s)`,
+  );
+  const updatedAutoPr = await db.pullRequestCheck.findUnique({
+    where: { id: autoRegressionPr.id },
+    select: { status: true, testsRun: true, regressions: true, impactJson: true, logsJson: true },
+  });
+  const updatedAutoImpact = jsonArray(updatedAutoPr?.impactJson) as unknown;
+  const autoImpactRecord = (() => {
+    try {
+      return JSON.parse(String(updatedAutoPr?.impactJson ?? "{}")) as Record<string, unknown>;
+    } catch {
+      return {} as Record<string, unknown>;
+    }
+  })();
+  void updatedAutoImpact;
+  const autoStoredRun = autoImpactRecord.autoRegression as
+    | { targetUrl?: string; passed?: number; failed?: number; total?: number; executed?: Array<Record<string, unknown>> }
+    | undefined;
+  check(
+    "PART 9 PR auto-regression updates PullRequestCheck with counts and run metadata",
+    (updatedAutoPr?.status === "passed_baseline" || updatedAutoPr?.status === "regression_caught") &&
+      updatedAutoPr?.testsRun === autoRegressionBody.total &&
+      updatedAutoPr?.regressions === autoRegressionBody.failed &&
+      Boolean(autoStoredRun) &&
+      autoStoredRun?.targetUrl === autoRegressionBody.targetUrl &&
+      (autoStoredRun?.executed ?? []).every((executed) => isScreenshot(executed.screenshotBase64) || jsonArray(executed.logJson).every((line) => isScreenshot(line.screenshotBase64))),
+    `${updatedAutoPr?.status ?? "?"} · ${updatedAutoPr?.testsRun ?? 0} run · ${updatedAutoPr?.regressions ?? 0} regression(s)`,
+  );
+  const storedPrCases = await db.testCase.findMany({
+    where: { organisationId: cgiId, title: { startsWith: `PR #${autoRegressionNumber} ·` } },
+    select: { id: true, title: true, variablesJson: true, stepsJson: true },
+  });
+  check(
+    "PART 9 PR-specific cases persist their test type and target URL",
+    storedPrCases.length === 2 &&
+      storedPrCases.every((testCase) => {
+        const variables = (() => {
+          try {
+            return JSON.parse(testCase.variablesJson || "{}") as Record<string, unknown>;
+          } catch {
+            return {} as Record<string, unknown>;
+          }
+        })();
+        return typeof variables._testType === "string" && typeof variables.url === "string" && variables.url.includes("cgi.com");
+      }),
+    storedPrCases.map((testCase) => testCase.title).join(" | ").slice(0, 180),
+  );
+  const prCaseRuns = await Promise.all(
+    storedPrCases.map((testCase) =>
+      db.testCaseRun.findFirst({
+        where: { testCaseId: testCase.id },
+        orderBy: { runNumber: "desc" },
+        select: { logJson: true },
+      }),
+    ),
+  );
+  check(
+    "PART 9 PR-specific case runs store screenshots in TestCaseRun.logJson",
+    prCaseRuns.length === 2 && prCaseRuns.every((run) => jsonArray(run?.logJson).length > 0 && jsonArray(run?.logJson).every((line) => isScreenshot(line.screenshotBase64))),
+    `${prCaseRuns.map((run) => jsonArray(run?.logJson).length).join("/")} line(s)`,
+  );
+
+  await goto(page, "/dashboard/pull-requests");
+  const autoRegressionRow = page.getByRole("button", { name: new RegExp(`#${autoRegressionNumber}`) }).first();
+  check("PART 9 the pull-request list shows the auto-regression PR", (await autoRegressionRow.count()) > 0, `#${autoRegressionNumber}`);
+  if ((await autoRegressionRow.count()) > 0) {
+    await autoRegressionRow.click().catch(() => undefined);
+    const autoDrawer = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "PR analysis detail" });
+    await autoDrawer.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 10_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    const autoSection = autoDrawer.locator('[data-testid="pr-auto-regression"]').first();
+    const autoEnvironmentSelect = autoDrawer.locator('[data-testid="pr-auto-regression-environment"]').first();
+    const autoEnvironmentOptions = await autoEnvironmentSelect.locator("option").count().catch(() => 0);
+    check(
+      "PART 9 PR drawer exposes auto-regression with an environment selector",
+      (await autoSection.count()) > 0 && (await autoEnvironmentSelect.count()) > 0 && autoEnvironmentOptions >= 1,
+      `${autoEnvironmentOptions} environment option(s)`,
+    );
+    const autoRunButton = autoDrawer.locator('[data-testid="pr-auto-regression-run"]').first();
+    check("PART 9 PR drawer exposes Run PR auto-regression", (await autoRunButton.count()) > 0);
+    if ((await autoRunButton.count()) > 0) {
+      await autoRunButton.click().catch(() => undefined);
+      const autoResult = autoDrawer.locator('[data-testid="pr-auto-regression-result"]').first();
+      await autoResult.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 120_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+      const autoResultText = ((await autoResult.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+      check(
+        "PART 9 the PR drawer renders auto-regression results",
+        (await autoResult.count()) > 0 &&
+          /passed/i.test(autoResultText) &&
+          autoResultText.includes(String(autoRegressionBody.targetUrl ?? "")),
+        autoResultText.slice(0, 180),
+      );
+    }
+    await autoDrawer.getByRole("button", { name: "Close", exact: true }).first().click().catch(() => undefined);
+    await page.waitForTimeout(300);
+  }
+  await db.pullRequestCheck.delete({ where: { id: autoRegressionPr.id } }).catch(() => undefined);
+  for (const testCase of storedPrCases) await apiJson(page, `/api/dashboard/test-cases/${testCase.id}`, "DELETE");
+
+  // 9.6 — Super Admin enterprise provisioning + plan configuration.
+  const part9Super = await loginAs(browser, "admin@qasyn.com", "QASynMaster2026!");
+  let provisionedOrgId = "";
+  try {
+    const provisionName = `PART9 Enterprise ${RUN_ID}`;
+    const provisionEmail = `part9-${RUN_ID}@qasyn.example`;
+    const provisionPassword = "Part9Demo2026!";
+    const provisionRes = await apiJson(part9Super.page, "/api/dashboard/super-admin", "POST", {
+      action: "create_enterprise_tenant",
+      organisationName: provisionName,
+      website: "https://www.cgi.com/part9-enterprise",
+      adminName: "Part9 Admin",
+      adminEmail: provisionEmail,
+      adminPassword: provisionPassword,
+      seats: 12,
+      environmentName: "Production",
+      environmentUrl: "https://www.cgi.com/part9-production",
+      trialCredits: 250000,
+    });
+    interface Part9ProvisionBody {
+      organisation?: { id?: string; name?: string; slug?: string; plan?: string; trialCredits?: number };
+      admin?: { id?: string; email?: string; role?: string; status?: string };
+      environments?: Array<{ id?: string; name?: string; baseUrl?: string; isDefault?: boolean }>;
+      ledger?: { id?: string; credits?: number; kind?: string };
+    }
+    const provisionBody = provisionRes.body as unknown as Part9ProvisionBody;
+    provisionedOrgId = String(provisionBody.organisation?.id ?? "");
+    check(
+      "PART 9 Super Admin provisions an enterprise tenant",
+      provisionRes.status === 201 &&
+        provisionBody.organisation?.plan === "enterprise" &&
+        provisionBody.organisation?.trialCredits === 250000 &&
+        Boolean(provisionedOrgId),
+      `${provisionBody.organisation?.name ?? "?"} · ${provisionBody.organisation?.plan ?? "?"} · ${provisionBody.organisation?.trialCredits ?? "?"} credits`,
+    );
+    check(
+      "PART 9 provisioning seeds Production + Staging environments, a credit ledger and an admin",
+      (provisionBody.environments ?? []).length === 2 &&
+        (provisionBody.environments ?? []).some((environment) => environment.isDefault && environment.name === "Production") &&
+        (provisionBody.environments ?? []).some((environment) => environment.name === "Staging") &&
+        provisionBody.ledger?.kind === "grant" &&
+        provisionBody.ledger?.credits === 250000 &&
+        provisionBody.admin?.role === "Admin" &&
+        provisionBody.admin?.status === "active",
+      `${(provisionBody.environments ?? []).map((environment) => environment.name).join(", ")} · ledger ${provisionBody.ledger?.credits ?? "?"}`,
+    );
+    const provisionedAdmin = await db.user.findUnique({
+      where: { email: provisionEmail },
+      select: { role: true, status: true, emailVerifiedAt: true, passwordHash: true, organisationId: true },
+    });
+    check(
+      "PART 9 the provisioned admin is verified and stores only a password hash",
+      provisionedAdmin?.role === "Admin" &&
+        provisionedAdmin?.status === "active" &&
+        Boolean(provisionedAdmin?.emailVerifiedAt) &&
+        provisionedAdmin?.passwordHash !== provisionPassword &&
+        String(provisionedAdmin?.passwordHash ?? "").length > 20 &&
+        provisionedAdmin?.organisationId === provisionedOrgId,
+      `${provisionedAdmin?.role ?? "?"} · verified=${Boolean(provisionedAdmin?.emailVerifiedAt)}`,
+    );
+    const provisionAudit = await db.systemAuditLog.findFirst({
+      where: { organisationId: provisionedOrgId, action: "super_admin.enterprise_tenant_created" },
+      select: { id: true },
+    });
+    check("PART 9 provisioning records a system audit entry", Boolean(provisionAudit));
+
+    const configureRes = await apiJson(part9Super.page, "/api/dashboard/super-admin", "POST", {
+      action: "configure_enterprise_plan",
+      organisationId: provisionedOrgId,
+      plan: "enterprise",
+      grantCredits: 5000,
+      seats: 30,
+      environmentName: "Production",
+      environmentUrl: "https://www.cgi.com/part9-staging",
+      ssoEnabled: true,
+      slaEnabled: true,
+    });
+    interface Part9ConfigureBody {
+      organisation?: { id?: string; plan?: string; trialCredits?: number };
+      environment?: { name?: string; baseUrl?: string; isDefault?: boolean } | null;
+      ledger?: { credits?: number; kind?: string } | null;
+      enterprise?: { seats?: number; ssoEnabled?: boolean; slaEnabled?: boolean };
+    }
+    const configureBody = configureRes.body as unknown as Part9ConfigureBody;
+    check(
+      "PART 9 Super Admin configures the enterprise plan and grants credits",
+      configureRes.status === 200 &&
+        configureBody.organisation?.plan === "enterprise" &&
+        configureBody.organisation?.trialCredits === 255000 &&
+        configureBody.ledger?.credits === 5000 &&
+        configureBody.ledger?.kind === "grant" &&
+        configureBody.enterprise?.seats === 30 &&
+        configureBody.enterprise?.ssoEnabled === true &&
+        configureBody.enterprise?.slaEnabled === true,
+      `${configureBody.organisation?.plan ?? "?"} · ${configureBody.organisation?.trialCredits ?? "?"} credits · ${configureBody.enterprise?.seats ?? "?"} seats`,
+    );
+    check(
+      "PART 9 configuring the tenant updates its default deployed environment",
+      configureBody.environment?.name === "Production" &&
+        configureBody.environment?.baseUrl === "https://www.cgi.com/part9-staging" &&
+        configureBody.environment?.isDefault === true,
+      `${configureBody.environment?.name ?? "?"} · ${configureBody.environment?.baseUrl ?? "?"}`,
+    );
+
+    await goto(part9Super.page, "/dashboard/settings/organization");
+    const provisionButton = part9Super.page.locator('[data-testid="super-admin-provision-enterprise"]').first();
+    await provisionButton.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 12_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    check("PART 9 Super-Admin Console exposes Provision enterprise", (await provisionButton.count()) > 0);
+    if ((await provisionButton.count()) > 0) {
+      await provisionButton.click().catch(() => undefined);
+      const provisionDialog = part9Super.page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Provision an enterprise tenant" });
+      await provisionDialog.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 8_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+      const provisionDialogText = ((await provisionDialog.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+      check(
+        "PART 9 the Provision modal collects tenant, admin, seats, environment and credits",
+        (await provisionDialog.count()) > 0 &&
+          provisionDialogText.toLowerCase().includes("organisation") &&
+          provisionDialogText.toLowerCase().includes("admin e-mail") &&
+          provisionDialogText.toLowerCase().includes("seats") &&
+          provisionDialogText.toLowerCase().includes("environment url") &&
+          provisionDialogText.toLowerCase().includes("credits") &&
+          (await part9Super.page.locator('[data-testid="super-admin-provision-submit"]').count()) > 0,
+        provisionDialogText.slice(0, 180),
+      );
+      await provisionDialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Cancel", exact: true }).first().click().catch(() => undefined);
+      await part9Super.page.waitForTimeout(300);
+    }
+    const configureOpen = part9Super.page.locator(`[data-testid="super-admin-configure-open-${String(provisionBody.organisation?.slug ?? "")}"]`).first();
+    check("PART 9 Super-Admin Console exposes Configure for the new tenant", (await configureOpen.count()) > 0);
+    if ((await configureOpen.count()) > 0) {
+      await configureOpen.click().catch(() => undefined);
+      const configureDialog = part9Super.page.getByRole("dialog", { name: /Configure enterprise/ }).first();
+      await configureDialog.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 8_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+      const configureDialogText = ((await configureDialog.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+      check(
+        "PART 9 the Configure modal exposes plan, seats, SSO/SLA and default environment",
+        (await configureDialog.count()) > 0 &&
+          configureDialogText.includes("Plan") &&
+          configureDialogText.includes("Seats") &&
+          configureDialogText.includes("SSO") &&
+          configureDialogText.includes("SLA") &&
+          (await part9Super.page.locator('[data-testid="super-admin-configure-enterprise"]').count()) > 0,
+        configureDialogText.slice(0, 180),
+      );
+      await configureDialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Cancel", exact: true }).first().click().catch(() => undefined);
+    }
+  } finally {
+    await part9Super.context.close().catch(() => undefined);
+  }
+  if (provisionedOrgId) {
+    await db.user.deleteMany({ where: { organisationId: provisionedOrgId } }).catch(() => undefined);
+    await db.environmentConfig.deleteMany({ where: { organisationId: provisionedOrgId } }).catch(() => undefined);
+    await db.creditEntry.deleteMany({ where: { organisationId: provisionedOrgId } }).catch(() => undefined);
+    await db.systemAuditLog.deleteMany({ where: { organisationId: provisionedOrgId } }).catch(() => undefined);
+    await db.organisation.delete({ where: { id: provisionedOrgId } }).catch(() => undefined);
+  }
+
+  // 9.7 — Production README + interactive /guide.
+  const readme = fs.readFileSync(path.join(process.cwd(), "README.md"), "utf8");
+  const readmeSections = [
+    "## Architecture",
+    "## The 19 product modules",
+    "## The 11 test types",
+    "## Bivia: grounded decisions and self-healing",
+    "## PR gatekeeper and auto-regression",
+    "## Super Admin and multi-tenancy",
+    "## Evidence: screenshots for every executed step",
+    "## Installation",
+    "## Environment variables",
+    "## Seeding and demo accounts",
+    "## Running and verification",
+  ];
+  check(
+    "PART 9 README documents architecture, modules, test types, Bivia, PR gate, Super Admin and operations",
+    readme.includes("# QASyn") && readmeSections.every((section) => readme.includes(section)),
+    `${readme.length} chars · ${readmeSections.filter((section) => !readme.includes(section)).join(", ") || "all sections"}`,
+  );
+
+  await goto(page, "/guide");
+  const guideBody = ((await body(page)) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+  const guideRoleTabs = await page.locator("[data-guide-role]").count();
+  const guideFeatures = await page.locator("[data-guide-feature]").count();
+  const guideWalkthroughs = await page.locator('[data-testid="guide-walkthrough"]').count();
+  check(
+    "PART 9 /guide serves the interactive user guide",
+    (await page.locator('[data-testid="user-guide"]').count()) > 0 &&
+      guideBody.includes("QASyn") &&
+      (await page.locator('[data-testid="guide-role-tabs"]').count()) > 0 &&
+      (await page.locator('[data-testid="guide-feature-explorer"]').count()) > 0 &&
+      guideRoleTabs === 6 &&
+      guideFeatures >= 19 &&
+      guideWalkthroughs === 6,
+    `${guideRoleTabs} role tab(s) · ${guideFeatures} feature(s) · ${guideWalkthroughs} walkthrough(s)`,
+  );
+  const guideSearch = page.locator('[data-testid="guide-feature-search"]').first();
+  if ((await guideSearch.count()) > 0) {
+    await guideSearch.fill("screenshot").catch(() => undefined);
+    await page.waitForTimeout(250);
+    const visibleFeatures = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll("[data-guide-feature]:not([hidden])").length);
+    const featureCountText = ((await page.locator("#feature-count").innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").trim();
+    check(
+      "PART 9 the feature explorer is searchable",
+      visibleFeatures > 0 && visibleFeatures < guideFeatures && /feature/i.test(featureCountText),
+      `${visibleFeatures}/${guideFeatures} visible · ${featureCountText}`,
+    );
+    await guideSearch.fill("").catch(() => undefined);
+  } else {
+    check("PART 9 the feature explorer is searchable", false, "search input missing");
+  }
+  const qaRoleTab = page.locator('[data-guide-role="QA"]').first();
+  if ((await qaRoleTab.count()) > 0) {
+    await qaRoleTab.click().catch(() => undefined);
+    await page.waitForTimeout(250);
+    const roleTitle = ((await page.locator("#role-title").innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").trim();
+    check(
+      "PART 9 role tabs focus the guide on the selected role",
+      (await qaRoleTab.getAttribute("aria-selected").catch(() => "")) === "true" && roleTitle === "QA",
+      `${roleTitle || "?"} · aria-selected=${await qaRoleTab.getAttribute("aria-selected").catch(() => "?")}`,
+    );
+  } else {
+    check("PART 9 role tabs focus the guide on the selected role", false, "QA tab missing");
+  }
+  const guideDrawerButton = page.locator('[data-testid="guide-architecture"]').first();
+  if ((await guideDrawerButton.count()) > 0) {
+    await guideDrawerButton.click().catch(() => undefined);
+    const guideDrawer = page.locator('[data-testid="guide-architecture-drawer"]').first();
+    await guideDrawer.waitFor({ state: "attached", timeout: 5_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    const drawerArchitectureText = ((await guideDrawer.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+    const guideApiTab = page.locator('[data-drawer-tab="api"]').first();
+    if ((await guideApiTab.count()) > 0) await guideApiTab.click().catch(() => undefined);
+    await page.waitForTimeout(250);
+    const guideApiText = ((await guideDrawer.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+    check(
+      "PART 9 the architecture / API drawer documents the run pipeline and delivery endpoints",
+      (await guideDrawer.getAttribute("aria-hidden").catch(() => "true")) === "false" &&
+        drawerArchitectureText.includes("Run pipeline") &&
+        drawerArchitectureText.includes("Grounding priority") &&
+        guideApiText.includes("/api/dashboard/pull-requests/auto-regression") &&
+        guideApiText.includes("/api/dashboard/super-admin"),
+      guideApiText.slice(0, 180),
+    );
+    await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
+    await page.waitForTimeout(250);
+  } else {
+    check("PART 9 the architecture / API drawer documents the run pipeline and delivery endpoints", false, "drawer button missing");
+  }
+  const guideResponse = await page.evaluate(async () => {
+    const response = await fetch("/guide");
+    return { status: response.status, contentType: response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "", text: await response.text() };
+  });
+  check(
+    "PART 9 /guide is a self-contained HTML route with role tabs, search and six walkthroughs",
+    guideResponse.status === 200 &&
+      guideResponse.contentType.includes("text/html") &&
+      guideResponse.text.includes("data-guide-role") &&
+      guideResponse.text.includes("guide-feature-search") &&
+      (guideResponse.text.match(/data-testid="guide-walkthrough"/g) ?? []).length === 6,
+    `${guideResponse.status} · ${guideResponse.contentType} · ${guideResponse.text.length} chars`,
+  );
+  await goto(page, "/dashboard/overview");
+  check("PART 9 the sidebar links to the interactive user guide", (await page.locator('a[href="/guide"]').count()) > 0);
+  check("PART 9 has no page errors", moduleErrors.length === 0, moduleErrors.slice(0, 2).join(" | "));
+  await shot(page, "part9-deliverables");
+
   await db.$disconnect();
 
   // ── Global integrity ────────────────────────────────────────────
